We show that they are all derived from a single geometric object induced by every differentiable embedding, whether defined implicitly through optimization or explicitly by a learned mapping. This framework provides two complementary geometric views of an embedding. The differential view explains local behavior: its first-order term recovers projection glyphs, while its second-order curvature quantifies how far their linear approximation remains reliable. The integral view follows the same geometry along high dimensional paths and determines whether an embedding depends only on the current state or also on the path taken to reach it. We further show that map-continuity is a prerequisite for the other analyses. The framework is theoretically complete for diagnostics derived from the embedding geometry, and we prove the integral view irreducible: no amount of local measurement at any number of points, to any order of derivative, reproduces what it detects. Classical rank-based metrics form a complementary class based on finite-scale neighborhood relationships. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets validate theoretical predictions, demonstrate accurate curvature-based trust estimates on single-cell embeddings, and show that the integral analysis distinguishes single-valued embeddings from path-dependent optimization-based embeddings in ways that existing pointwise diagnostics cannot.

</details>


### [164] [Multiscale Reward Hedging from Correct Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06825)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为连续奖励类别提供了无horizon的在线学习保证（从正确示范中学习，而非监督学习）。核心创新是通过在所有精度尺度上的容忍最优性检验中共享单一投票进行对冲，得到同时尾部界和度量熵积分形式的累积隐藏差距界。在多项式熵条件下达到O(d log A)的总差距和O(d/m)的统计速率；对线性上下文推荐给出任意紧致菜单的O(d)遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从正确示范中学习比监督学习更困难，因为学习者在预测后只能看到一个有效答案，却无法得知自己的答案是否有效，也没有奖励信号。现有的奖励对冲保证仅适用于有限奖励类别，尚无针对连续类别的horizon-free（无回合数依赖）保证。

Method: 核心方法是在每个精度尺度上的容忍最优性检验（tolerant optimality test）中共享单一投票进行对冲。每个目标奖励在每个尺度上保留一个代理，当预测的gap超过该尺度时将该代理加倍。由此得到同时尾部界：|{t: ℓ_t > 2^(-j)}| ≤ log2 N(G, 2^(-j-1)) + j，其中G是最优性gap函数类。对尾部积分得到由度量熵积分界定的累积隐藏gap，与轮数无关。对一维Lipschitz参数曲线，一般投票可精确多项式时间计算；固定半径的rank-two推荐需O(KT²)时间。

Result: 多项式熵(A/ε)^d下总gap为O(d log A)，统计速率为O(d/m)。对线性上下文推荐任意紧致菜单得到O(d)遗憾界，这是首个无需菜单结构限制的多项式有限界（代价是预测不当）。还证明了Ω(d)下界、低秩和ReLU网络推论，以及加入示范者累积次优性的鲁棒定理。MovieLens实验在10个用户上1.7秒完成因子分解审计，改善了平均潜在gap。

Conclusion: 本文为连续类别的正确示范学习提供了首个hindsight-free（无horizon依赖）的理论保证，通过共享投票对冲机制绕过了有限奖励类别假设的限制。实验验证了尺度自适应行为和实际可用性，同时承认一般投票可能计算开销大，但在一维Lipschitz曲线等特例下可多项式时间实现。

Abstract: Learning from correct demonstrations is harder than supervised learning when many answers are correct: after predicting, the learner sees one valid answer but not whether its own answer was valid, nor any reward. Existing reward-hedging guarantees consequently assume a finite reward class. We give the first horizon-free guarantee for continuous classes. The key is to hedge in one shared vote over tolerant optimality tests at every accuracy scale. A target reward has one surviving proxy per scale, and a prediction with gap above that scale doubles the proxy. This yields the simultaneous tail bound $|\{t:\ell_t>2^{-j}\}|\leq \log_2\mathcal N(\mathcal G,2^{-j-1})+j$, where $\mathcal G$ is the class of optimality-gap functions. Integrating the tails gives cumulative hidden gap bounded by a metric-entropy integral, independently of the number of rounds. Polynomial entropy $(A/ε)^d$ gives $O(d\log A)$ total gap and a fast $O(d/m)$ statistical rate. For bounded linear contextual recommendation, the result is $O(d)$ regret for arbitrary compact menus. This is the first polynomial finite bound without structural restrictions on the menus, at the price of improper prediction. Although the general vote can be expensive, it is exactly polynomial-time for one-dimensional Lipschitz parameter curves. Fixed-radius rank-two recommendation takes $O(KT^2)$ time for menus of size $K$. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ lower bound, low-rank and bounded ReLU-network corollaries, and a robust theorem that adds only the demonstrator's cumulative suboptimality. A reproducible adaptive stress test illustrates the predicted scale adaptation. After factorization, an exact MovieLens audit runs in 1.7 CPU seconds across ten users and improves mean latent gap over both a demonstrated-rating policy and a proper online baseline. The learner uses only action demonstrations and never observes a reward or a loss.

</details>


### [165] [Graph Machine: Exploring Edge Mechanisms as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06834)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph Machine架构，通过边增强注意力和边中心推荐两种显式边机制为Transformer引入迭代遍历潜在关系的归纳偏置，在数独任务上超越Transformer基线，并发现模型自动学习出紧凑的基于边的数独几何构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer擅长全局内容匹配，但推理问题可能需要更强的归纳偏置来支持对潜在关系的迭代遍历。作者认为显式边机制能够帮助模型动态构建和修正关系图，从而提升推理能力。

Method: 提出Graph Machine架构，包含两种显式边机制：(1) 边增强注意力（Edge-augmented attention），边信息调制节点间的注意力权重；(2) 边中心推荐（Edge-centric referral），节点之间交换地址信息以更新边。该模型可在层间动态、可微地构建和修订关系图。

Result: 在受控的数独设置下，Graph Machine优于Transformer基线；消融实验和机制分析表明性能提升归因于边机制；模型还意外发现了一种紧凑的基于边的数独几何构造方式。

Conclusion: 显式边机制是一种有前景的架构设计方向，值得更广泛的评估与应用。

Abstract: Transformers provide a powerful architecture for global content-based matching, but reasoning problems may benefit from a stronger inductive bias toward iterative traversal of latent relations. We introduce Graph Machine, an architecture with two explicit edge-based mechanisms: Edge-augmented attention, in which edges modulate attention between nodes, and edge-centric referral, in which nodes exchange addresses to update their edges. Conceptually, this enables the model to dynamically and differentiably construct and revise relational graphs across layers. We study this inductive bias using Sudoku under controlled settings and find that Graph Machine outperforms Transformer baselines, with ablation studies and mechanistic analysis attributing the gains to the edge mechanisms. Surprisingly, we found that the model discovers a compact edge-based construction for Sudoku geometry. Our results support explicit edge mechanisms as a promising architectural design, motivating broader evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [Mathematical Principles and Experimental Discoveries of the Emergence of Symbolic Patterns in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06839)
*Quanshi Zhang,Qihan Ren,Siyu Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类广泛的神经网络（ANN）的推理逻辑可以被重构为稀疏符号交互模式，并证明了两个通用的数学准则促成了这种符号模式的涌现。实验证实了这些交互的可信度、可迁移性及其对ANN泛化能力的解释力，并提出了"交流式学习"这一新型学习范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络常被视为黑盒模型，可解释性一直是深度学习的核心挑战。虽已有众多工程方法从特征归因和可视化等角度近似解释ANN，但ANN复杂推理逻辑能否被穷尽且简洁地表示为稀疏符号模式，仍是一个长期悬而未决的问题。本文旨在回答这一问题，并探究符号模式的涌现是否反映自然规律而非偶然。

Method: 本文首先提出ANN的推理逻辑可以被重构为稀疏符号交互，然后从理论上证明两个通用的数学准则（跨任务隐式要求）是符号交互涌现的充分条件。通过实证验证这两个准则在多种模型的大多数输入样本上成立，并通过样本间与模型间的可迁移性以及其对泛化能力的解释力来验证符号交互的可信度（faithfulness）。证明不依赖任何特定ANN架构。

Result: 实证结果表明：两个数学准则在多种模型的多数输入样本上成立；所提取的稀疏符号交互具有较强的样本间和模型间可迁移性；这些交互能够解释ANN的整体泛化能力。广泛的实验为ANN的符号解释提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: ANN的推理逻辑确实可以以稀疏符号交互的形式进行穷尽且简洁的解释，这种符号模式的涌现反映了一种自然规律。该工作提出了"交流式学习"范式，允许在符号模式层面直接检查和调整ANN的推理逻辑，补充了传统的端到端学习范式。由于证明不依赖特定架构，该发现暗示其他黑盒系统在特定条件下也可能涌现类似的符号表示。

Abstract: Artificial Neural networks (ANNs) are often treated as black-box models, making explainability a central challenge in deep learning. Many engineering methods have been proposed to approximately explain the ANN from various perspectives, such as feature attribution and visualization. However, it remains a long-standing open question whether the complex inference logic of an ANN can be explained exhaustively and concisely as sparse symbolic patterns. This raises a deeper inquiry: does the emergence of symbolic patterns reflect a natural law rather than chance? Here, we show that across a broad class of ANNs trained on diverse tasks, their inference logic can indeed be reformulated as sparse symbolic interactions. We further prove that two common mathematical criteria, which are implicitly required across tasks, lead to the emergence of such sparse symbolic interactions. Empirical evidence confirms that the two criteria hold for the majority of input samples in diverse models. Furthermore, the faithfulness of these interactions is also demonstrated by their strong sample-to-sample and model-to-model transferability, as well as their ability to explain the overall generalization power of ANNs. Our theoretical analysis and extensive experiments provide a solid foundation for symbolic explanations of ANNs, and offer novel insights into the ANN's generalization power. Our findings also highlight the potential of communicative learning, a paradigm in which the inference logic of an ANN can be directly inspected and tuned at the level of symbolic patterns, thus complementing traditional end-to-end learning paradigm. Finally, the observed emergence of symbolic patterns in ANNs suggests that similar symbolic representations may also emerge in other types of black-box systems under certain conditions, because our proof does not depend on any specific ANN architecture.

</details>


### [167] [Bridging the Gap Between Hyperdimensional Computing and Kernel Methods via the Nyström Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06860)
*Quanling Zhao,Anthony Hitchcock Thomas,Ari Brin,Xiaofan Yu,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NysHD方法，基于Nyström核近似技术，将任意用户定义的正半定相似函数转化为超维计算（HDC）中的高维映射，从而扩展HDC可处理的问题类型。实验表明，与现有HDC编码方法相比，NysHD在图和字符串数据集上分别平均提升11%和17%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超维计算（HDC）的有效性很大程度上取决于原始数据如何映射到高维空间，但现有编码方法类型有限，限制了HDC可解决的问题范围。需要一种通用机制，将核方法领域中丰富的相似函数设计引入HDC框架。

Method: 基于Nyström核近似方法，提出NysHD编码方案。该方法提供一个简单流程，将任何用户定义的正半定相似函数转化为HDC中的高维随机向量映射，从而将核近似理论中的相似函数设计成果导入HDC设置中。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实证评估表明，NysHD与现有HDC编码方法相比，在图形数据集上平均分类准确率提升11%，在字符串数据集上平均提升17%。

Conclusion: NysHD通过桥接核方法与HDC，为HDC引入了更广泛的相似性函数选择，显著扩展了HDC在机器学习中的应用范围，并取得了优于现有编码方法的分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) is an approach from the cognitive science literature for solving information processing tasks using data represented as high-dimensional random vectors. The technique has a rigorous mathematical backing, and is easy to implement in energy-efficient and highly parallel hardware like FPGAs and "processing-in-memory" architectures. The effectiveness of HDC in machine learning largely depends on how raw data is mapped to high-dimensional space. In this work, we propose NysHD, a new method for constructing this mapping that is based on the Nyström method from the literature on kernel approximation. Our approach provides a simple recipe to turn any user-defined positive-semidefinite similarity function into an equivalent mapping in HDC. There is a vast literature on the design of such functions for learning problems. Our approach provides a mechanism to import them into the HDC setting, expanding the types of problems that can be tackled using HDC. Empirical evaluation against existing HDC encoding methods shows that NysHD can achieve, on average, 11% and 17% better classification accuracy on graph and string datasets respectively.

</details>


### [168] [SkillAligner: Treating Retrieved Skills as Adaptable Drafts at Execution Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06880)
*Qinfeng Li,Dalin He,Yuntai Bao,Ying Yang,Ruoxi Chen,Xinyan Yu,Lizhou Liang,Ge Su,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillAligner是一个无需训练的、在技能执行前进行一次性联合适配的框架，将检索到的技能视为可改编的草稿而非固定指令，通过专化有用技能片段、对齐程序性假设与可用执行接口、解决技能间冲突与冗余，生成紧凑的执行指南以提升任务性能并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用技能承诺为语言智能体提供可复用的程序性知识，但语义相关性并不能保证执行效用：检索到的技能可能包含与当前任务、执行环境或其他技能冲突的假设，导致技能-执行错配问题。现有方法直接将检索到的技能作为固定指令执行，未考虑这种结构性不匹配。

Method: SkillAligner采用训练无关的执行时技能适配框架，将检索技能视为可改编草稿。在执行前进行一次联合适配：(1)将有用的技能片段专化到当前任务需求；(2)对齐技能中的程序性假设与可用的执行接口；(3)通过解析技能间的依赖、冲突和冗余来组合生成的指导。最终将适配内容整合为紧凑的执行指南，并在后续整个轨迹中复用。

Result: 在多个多样的智能体基准和模型骨干上的大量实验表明，SkillAligner相较于现有技能使用基线显著提升了任务性能，在实例级别减少了技能导致的性能回退，并降低了总推理成本。

Conclusion: 技能的有效利用不仅取决于语义相关性，还需要在执行层面对技能进行适应性调整。SkillAligner证明了对检索技能进行执行时联合适配（而非直接使用）是提升语言智能体性能并降低推理开销的有效策略。

Abstract: General-purpose skills promise reusable procedural knowledge for language agents, yet semantic relevance does not guarantee execution utility: a retrieved skill may encode assumptions that conflict with the current task, execution environment, or other retrieved skills. We formalize this problem as the skill--execution misfit. To address it, we propose SkillAligner, a training-free execution-time skill adaptation framework that treats retrieved skills as adaptable drafts rather than fixed instructions. Before execution, SkillAligner performs a one-time joint adaptation that specializes useful skill fragments to task requirements, aligns their procedural assumptions with the available execution interface, and composes the resulting guidance by resolving dependencies, conflicts, and redundancy across skills. The adapted content is consolidated into a compact execution guide and reused throughout the subsequent trajectory. Extensive experiments across diverse agent benchmarks and model backbones show that SkillAligner substantially improves task performance over existing skill-use baselines, reduces skill-induced regressions at the instance level, and lowers total inference cost.

</details>


### [169] [PRISM: Principled Reference Identification for Schrodinger Bridge Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06893)
*Forouzan Fallah,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM提出了桥梁参考过程设计的系统理论，在Gaussian框架下精确刻画了可解的时变参考过程，证明了在无限计算资源下所有合法参考等价，并推导出有限步预算下的最优噪声谱的闭式解。实验验证了理论预测，并定位了真实图像数据中理论失效的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schrödinger桥模型在从退化观测中恢复干净信号时，参考过程的选择（通常为白噪声及其调度）是启发式的、靠人工调参决定，缺乏理论基础。参考过程的选择极大地影响模型的性能与训练成本，因此需要一套系统的参考设计理论来取代经验性的超参数搜索。

Method: 1) 理论刻画：证明时变Gaussian参考中精确可解的充要条件是其瞬时协方差矩阵可交换（commute），并给出按模式（per-mode）调度表达。2) 不可见性原理：证明在精确漂移和无限求解步数下，所有合法参考都恢复真实后验分布。3) 有限步分析：在固定步数预算下推导闭式有限步目标函数，证明最优噪声谱正比于传感器破坏信息谱Pk，常数项为(2 ln T)^-1/2(1+o(1))。4) 理论扩展：证明噪声颜色与时间调度可互换，且正则化使最优参考偏向白噪声。5) 实验验证：在Gaussian设定验证预测排序与闭式损失下限，并在FFHQ真实图像数据集上进行失真-感知权衡分析及机制归因研究。

Result: 在Gaussian设定下，理论预测的顺序和闭式损失下限得到实验验证。在FFHQ上，失真-感知权衡与谱定位结论仍然成立，但白噪声优于匹配参考；预注册研究排除了岭白化作为解释。2x2机制研究将这一反转归因于真实图像的非Gaussian每模式统计量。

Conclusion: PRISM将桥梁参考设计从超参数扫描转化为Gaussian框架下的闭式计算，并精确定位了真实图像数据中该理论失效的位置——即非Gaussian的每模式统计数据，这为未来桥模型的统一设计提供了理论基线和边界认知。

Abstract: Schrödinger bridge models restore a clean signal from a degraded observation by following the conditional bridges of a reference process, yet this reference is chosen heuristically, typically white noise with a hand-tuned schedule. We develop PRISM, a theory of bridge reference design. We characterize the time-varying Gaussian references that remain exactly tractable with per-mode schedules: precisely those whose instantaneous covariances commute. We then prove an invisibility principle: with the exact drift and unlimited solver steps, every admissible reference recovers the true posterior. The choice of reference therefore matters only under finite computational resources. For a fixed step budget, we derive the finite-step objective in closed form and prove that every optimal noise spectrum is proportional to Pk, the spectrum of information destroyed by the sensor, with a mode-independent constant x*(T) = (2 ln T)^-1/2 (1 + o(1)). The analysis shows that noise color and temporal scheduling are interchangeable, and regularization provably shifts the optimal reference toward white noise. Experiments in Gaussian settings confirm the predicted orderings and the closed-form loss floors. On FFHQ, the distortion-- perception trade-off and spectral localization transfer, but white noise outperforms the matched reference; a pre-registered study that changes the training regime refutes ridge whitening as the explanation. A 2x2 mechanism study then traces the inversion to the non-Gaussian per-mode statistics of real images. PRISM turns reference design from a hyperparameter sweep into a calculation in the Gaussian regime, and locates exactly where real images break it.

</details>


### [170] [Recent advances in weakly supervised learning: New supervision paradigms, assumption relaxations, and practical solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06896)
*Wei Wang,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了弱监督学习的最新进展，涵盖三个具体方向：置信度差异二分类、互补标签学习（多分类）和部分标签学习的评估框架，针对真实世界中训练数据不完整、不精确或不准确的问题提出解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习依赖高质量、完整标注的训练数据，但真实应用中往往难以满足此要求。弱监督学习旨在利用不完整、不精确或不准确的监督信息训练出准确模型，是解决数据标注瓶颈的关键方向。

Method: 1) 提出置信度差异二分类问题及其一致性的求解方法；2) 提出基于更宽松数据生成过程假设的互补标签学习一致性方法；3) 构建部分标签学习的评估框架以促进公平真实的算法评估。

Result: 分别针对三种弱监督学习任务提出了新的监督范式、更放松的假设条件以及系统的评估框架，在理论和实践层面推动了弱监督学习的发展。

Conclusion: 弱监督学习通过引入新监督范式、放宽假设和建立公平评估框架，能够在监督信息不完美的条件下训练出准确的模型，为实际应用提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved great success in recent years thanks to the availability of high-quality, well-annotated training data. However, this requirement is often not met in real-world applications. Weakly supervised learning aims to train an accurate model with incomplete, inexact, or inaccurate supervision. In this chapter, we will discuss recent advances in this field, including new supervision paradigms, relaxed assumptions, and practical solutions. First, we introduce a new weakly supervised binary classification problem called confidence-difference classification and propose consistent approaches to solve it. Next, we investigate complementary-label learning, a weakly supervised multi-class classification problem. Our proposed approaches are based on more relaxed assumptions about the data generation process than existing consistent approaches. Lastly, we present an evaluation framework for partial-label learning, another popular multi-class weakly supervised learning problem, in order to promote fair and realistic evaluation of algorithms in this field.

</details>


### [171] [MiCoPro: End-to-End Mixed Precision HW/SW Co-design with HW-aware Proxy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06916)
*Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiCo/MiCoPro框架，用于边缘AI场景下层式混合精度量化（MPQ）的探索与部署。该框架通过新的优化算法搜索延迟约束下的精度最优量化配置，并引入硬件感知代理（HAP）模型提高预测精度和硬件适应性，支持从PyTorch模型直接部署到裸机C代码。在BitFusion加速器和RISC-V处理器上实现了高达40%的延迟降低，精度损失小于3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层式混合精度量化（MPQ）算法在灵活性和效率上受限，难以理解不同MPQ方案对后训练量化和量化感知训练结果的复杂影响，且缺乏端到端的模型优化与部署框架。

Method: 提出MiCo框架，包含：(1) 新颖的优化算法用于在严格延迟约束下搜索精度最优的量化配置；(2) 扩展为MiCoPro，引入硬件感知代理（HAP）模型以增强预测精度和硬件通用性；(3) 基于目标特定的延迟建模，支持从PyTorch模型快速探索并直接部署为裸机C代码。

Result: 在BitFusion加速器和SIMD扩展RISC-V处理器上验证了框架的通用性，实现高达40%的延迟降低，且精度下降小于3%。

Conclusion: MiCo/MiCoPro提供了一个完整且高效的混合精度量化探索与部署方案，有效解决了MPQ搜索灵活性和端到端部署缺失的问题，为边缘AI应用提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Quantized Neural Networks~(QNN) with low-bitwidth data have proven promising in efficient storage and computation on edge devices. To mitigate accuracy degradation while maximizing speedup, layer-wise mixed-precision quantization~(MPQ) becomes a popular solution. However, existing algorithms for exploring MPQ schemes are limited in flexibility and efficiency. Comprehending the complex impacts of different MPQ schemes on post-training quantization and quantization-aware training results is a challenge for conventional methods. Furthermore, an end-to-end framework for the optimization and deployment of MPQ models is missing in existing work.
  To address these challenges, we propose the MiCo framework, a holistic MPQ exploration and deployment framework for edge AI applications. The framework adopts a novel optimization algorithm to search for accuracy-optimal quantization configurations under strict latency constraints. We further extended the framework to MiCoPro, which introduces a robust Hardware-Aware Proxy (HAP) model to enhance prediction accuracy and hardware versatility. By leveraging target-specific latency modeling, MiCoPro enables rapid exploration and direct deployment from PyTorch models to bare-metal C code. We demonstrate the versatility of our framework on both the BitFusion accelerator and SIMD-extended RISC-V processors, achieving up to 40\% of latency reduction with less than 3\% of accuracy drop.

</details>


### [172] [Walkable to Whom? Capturing Subjective Variability in Walkability Perception Using Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06934)
*Moloud Damandeh,Meead Saberi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含29,870条步行适宜性评级的大规模数据集（来自1,196位受访者，覆盖澳大利亚城市、郊区和区域环境），并首次提出用户条件化的多模态深度学习框架，融合视觉特征与受访者个体属性来预测步行适宜性感知。研究发现人行道视角图像比匹配的街景图像获得显著更高的评级，且用户条件化模型相比仅图像基线将评级一致性提升65%，证明评估者的个体特征对预测具有关键价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的步行适宜性感知研究存在两个核心问题：一是将多样化的个体判断简化为聚合分数，隐含地假设所有人的感知是统一的；二是普遍使用车载街景图像，无法反映行人真实的视觉体验。这两点都导致对步行环境评估不够准确和包容。

Method: 1）构建大规模数据集：收集29,870条步行适宜性评级，来自1,196位受访者，关联人行道视角图像与个体属性；2）提出首个用户条件化多模态深度学习框架，融合视觉特征与受访者级别的个体表示；3）进行视角对比研究，比较人行道视角与匹配街景视角下图像评级的差异。

Result: 1）人行道视角图像获得的步行适宜性评级显著高于匹配的街景图像，说明图像来源是感知调查中重要的设计决策；2）用户条件化模型相比仅图像基线，与观测评级的秩一致性提升65%（二次加权卡帕系数从0.29提升至0.47）；3）证明评估者是谁（who）携带超出图像内容本身的预测信息。

Conclusion: 研究支持从聚合的、与观察者无关的步行适宜性评分范式转向能够代表多样化用户的个性化模型，从而实现对步行环境更具包容性的评估。图像视角选择和个体差异建模都应当成为步行环境感知研究的核心设计考量。

Abstract: Visual perception of walkability varies substantially across individuals, reflecting differences in personal characteristics, experiences, and preferences. Existing studies, however, often reduce these diverse judgements to aggregated scores, implicitly assuming uniform perception, and commonly rely on vehicle-mounted street-view imagery that does not reflect the pedestrian's visual experience. This paper introduces a dataset of 29,870 walkability ratings from 1,196 respondents, linking sidewalk-view imagery across urban, suburban, and regional Australian environments with individual rater attributes, and proposes the first user-conditioned multimodal deep learning framework for walkability perception, fusing visual features with respondent-level representations. A viewpoint-comparison study shows that sidewalk-view images receive significantly higher walkability ratings than matched street-view images, indicating that imagery source is a substantive design decision in perception surveys. The user-conditioned model improves rank agreement with observed ratings by 65% over an image-only baseline (quadratic weighted kappa 0.47 vs. 0.29), demonstrating that who is evaluating an environment carries predictive indication beyond image content alone. These findings support moving from aggregated, observer-independent walkability scores toward models that represent diverse users, enabling more inclusive assessment of pedestrian environments.

</details>


### [173] [ELMZip: Onboard Satellite Image Compression via Extreme Learning Machines for Efficient Downlink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06942)
*Woojin Cho,Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Steve Andreas Immanuel,Junhyuk Heo,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELMZip提出了一种基于极限学习机(ELM)和域分解策略的免分辨率神经表示框架，用于小卫星多光谱图像的高效片上压缩。该方法将拟合过程转化为凸最小二乘问题，仅需传输紧凑的输出权重，避免了反向传播和全网络参数传输，在保持高重建保真度的同时显著降低下行链路负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小卫星（如CubeSats）获取多光谱图像时面临下行数据量巨大和通信窗口受限的双重瓶颈。传统压缩方法难以适应多波段、多分辨率数据的非线性统计特性，而现有神经表示方法依赖迭代优化且需传输全部网络参数，计算开销大、不适合资源受限平台。

Method: ELMZip基于随机特征单层网络（极限学习机）构建，将神经表示拟合转化为凸最小二乘问题，避免昂贵的反向传播训练。采用域分解策略处理多分辨率数据，并通过非对称传输协议仅下行发送紧凑的输出权重而非全部网络参数。

Result: ELMZip在显著降低下行链路负载（仅传输紧凑输出权重）的同时，实现了相比传统神经表示方法更高的压缩效率，并保持了高重建保真度，支持即时图像重建。

Conclusion: ELMZip为资源受限的小卫星平台提供了一种高效、免分辨率的片上神经压缩方案，最大化数据回传量，推进了实时AI驱动的地球观测能力。

Abstract: The acquisition of multispectral imagery via small satellites (e.g., CubeSats) presents significant data downlink challenges due to high data volumes and restricted communication windows. While onboard image compression is critical to address this bottleneck, traditional methods often struggle to adapt to the nonlinear statistics of multi-band, multi-resolution data. To overcome these limitations, we propose ELMZip, a novel framework based on Extreme Learning Machines (ELM) and domain decomposition strategies for efficient, resolution-free onboard neural representation. ELMZip formulates the fitting process as a convex least-squares problem using random-feature single-layer networks, thereby eliminating the need for computationally expensive backpropagation. By adopting an asymmetric transmission protocol that sends only the compact output weights, the proposed method significantly reduces the downlink payload. Unlike previous neural representation approaches that rely on iterative optimization and require transmitting full network parameters, ELMZip achieves significant compression efficiency while maintaining high reconstruction fidelity. This capability enables immediate image reconstruction for analysis, allowing resource-constrained platforms to maximize data return and advancing real-time AI-powered Earth observation.

</details>


### [174] [A Rate Separation for Agnostic Direct Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06951)
*Mihir More,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究agnostic PAC学习中直接和（direct sum）的学习曲线与单实例学习曲线及组件数量r的关系。作者构造了两个反例类F和G，二者具有相同的单实例学习率（n^{-1/2}），但它们的直接和经验学习曲线不同，从而证明单实例学习率不能决定直接和学习速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanneke、Moran和Waknine（2024）提出了一个开放问题：agnostic PAC学习框架下，直接和C^r的学习曲线如何依赖于单实例学习曲线eps(n|C)和参数r？本文旨在回答这一依赖关系，特别是检验单实例学习率是否足以确定直接和学习速率。

Method: 构造两个具体的学习类：F为两个常量二元函数组成的类，G由零函数和恒等函数组成。通过分析这两个类各自的单实例学习曲线（均为n^{-1/2}阶）以及它们对应直接和学习曲线，对比二者的差异来论证单实例学习率无法决定直接和学习速率。

Result: 两个类F和G虽然具有相同阶数的单实例agnostic学习曲线（n^{-1/2}），但它们的直接和学习曲线表现出不同的行为，说明直接和学习速率不仅仅由单实例学习率决定。

Conclusion: 单实例学习率并不能决定直接和的agnostic学习速率，Hanneke等人提出的问题需要更精细的刻画参数来描述直接和学习曲线对r和单实例学习曲线的依赖关系。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, and Waknine \cite{HannekeMoranWaknine2024} asked how the agnostic PAC learning curve of the direct sum $C^r$ depends on the single-instance learning curve $\epsagn(n\mid C)$ and on $r$. We show that the single-instance learning rate does not determine the direct-sum rate. Let $\F$ be the class of the two constant binary functions and let $\G$ consist of the zero function and the identity function. Both classes have agnostic learning curve of order $n^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [175] [How Molecular Generative Models Organize Molecular Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06956)
*Raul Ortega-Ochoa,Tejs Vegge,Jens S. Bakander,Luis Mantilla Calderon,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Tonio Buonassisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将分子身份显式化并沿生成过程拉回（pullback），研究分子生成模型内部如何组织离散化学身份。发现模型内部存在固定的"保留剧目"（repertoire）分区，由分段常数区域构成，具有粗到细的重现边界，其组织方式取决于表示、身份约定、解码器随机性和比较指标。训练过程中局部化学组织趋于稳定，但邻域内分子身份数持续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型通常被视为输出采样的工具，其潜空间被用作化学空间导航的代理，但对模型内部如何排列离散化学身份知之甚少。理解这一内部组织对判断潜空间是否真正"可化学导航"至关重要。

Method: 将分子身份显式化并通过生成过程进行拉回（pullback），探测生成同一对象的区域，揭示模型内部保留剧目——一个固定分区，决定模型能（或不能）产生哪些对象。在三种分子生成架构上进行系统性分析，考察表示、身份约定、解码器随机性和坐标比较指标等变量。

Result: 研究发现内部保留剧目被组织成分段常数区域，以粗到细的边界分隔；组织方式依赖于所探测的表示、身份约定、解码器随机性以及坐标比较指标；训练期间局部化学组织趋于稳定，但邻域内分子身份数量持续变化。

Conclusion: 生成空间不能被直接假定为化学可导航的，其内部组织必须被刻画而非假设。在将生成空间用作化学导航工具之前，需要对模型内部表征的分子身份组织进行系统性表征。

Abstract: Generative models for matter are often evaluated as samplers over output representations, and their latent spaces are commonly used as proxies for navigating chemical space. Much less is known about how these models internally arrange discrete chemical identities within those representations. We study this arrangement by making molecular identity explicit and pulling it back through the generative process. Through these pullbacks we probe the regions that generate the same object, exposing the trained model's internal repertoire: a fixed partition that determines which objects (novel or not) the model can produce.
  Across three molecular generative architectures, we find that this repertoire is arranged into piecewise-constant regions separated by recurring coarse-to-fine boundaries. Its organization depends on the representation probed, the identity convention, decoder stochasticity, and the metric used to compare coordinates. During training, local chemical organization stabilizes while the number of distinct molecular identities represented within each neighborhood continues to change. Internal organization must therefore be characterized, rather than assumed, before a generative space can be treated as chemically navigable.

</details>


### [176] [Density-aware Hierarchical Clustering Based on Element-Categorized Connection Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06990)
*Yuning Yu,José Rodríguez-Piñeiro,Xuefeng Yin,Bin Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于元素分类连接子图的密度感知层次聚类方法（DHC-ECS），将层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类三者有效整合，通过引入考虑距离、KNN连接子图中元素分类、核密度估计和局部连通性的新型类间相似度度量，在异构基准数据集上相比基线方法（AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS）展现出更优的聚类精度和参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的层次聚类在计算类间相似度时仅依赖成对距离，忽略了数据集的密度变化和图中的结构连通性，导致聚类效果受限。本文旨在解决这一局限性，通过整合密度信息和图结构信息来提升层次聚类的性能。

Method: 提出DHC-ECS方法，核心是引入一种新型类间相似度度量，该度量不仅考虑距离，还同时考虑KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计以及子簇内部的局部连通性。该方法将层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类的优势有机结合。

Result: 在异构基准数据集上的广泛评估表明，DHC-ECS在聚类精度和参数鲁棒性方面均优于基线方法（包括AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS）。该方法在低维数据集上展现了利用局部密度和图结构化连通性（即顶点和边的对偶性）的巨大潜力，并有可能确定内在阈值以减少对人工参数调优的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过融合密度感知和图的顶点-边对偶结构信息，可以显著提升层次聚类的整体性能，同时降低对人工参数调优的依赖，为低维数据集的聚类分析提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental data mining technique for pattern recognition through unsupervised learning. Among various clustering methods, hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering stand out as representative approaches. For hierarchical clustering, it can be categorized into agglomerative and divisive modes to construct clusters in a recursive manner. The key aspect of both modes is the calculation of inter-cluster similarity, which determines whether to merge the sub-clusters into one cluster or divide a current cluster into sub-clusters. Traditionally, the similarity is derived from pairwise distances, often overlooking density variations and structural connectivity in graphs. To address this, we propose a density-aware hierarchical clustering method based on element-categorized connection subgraphs (DHC-ECS), which effectively integrates the hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering. Particularly, a novel inter-cluster similarity metric is introduced that considers not only distances but also the element categorization in the KNN connection subgraphs, kernel density estimation, and local connectivity within sub-clusters. Extensive evaluations on heterogeneous benchmark datasets demonstrate that DHC-ECS exhibits superior overall performance in terms of clustering accuracy and parameter robustness compared with the baseline methods (including AChameleon, RNN-DBSCAN, McDPC, and G-RMS). The work indicates the great potential of the proposed clustering algorithm for low-dimensional datasets by leveraging local density and graph-structured connectivity (i.e., the duality of vertices and edges), as well as the possibility to determine an intrinsic threshold, reducing the reliance on manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [177] [Beyond Foundation Models: Dimension-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Small-Data Representation Models for Cryocooler Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06993)
*Gregor Molan,Grafika Jati,Francesco Barchi,Andrea Acquaviva,Aljaž Osterman,Martin Molan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FSD-RM范式，针对工业/科学领域缺乏大规模数据的问题，不依赖大规模预训练，而是在小数据条件下利用容量控制的表示学习（CNN1D/LSTM/GRU/Transformer编码器）结合维度感知神经架构搜索，在低温冷却器遥测数据上实现竞争性的寿命预测性能，同时降低训练成本和模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练时间序列模型虽表现优异，但依赖丰富多样的数据，而这在工业与科学领域中往往不可得。作者旨在为数据受限的特定领域遥测数据提供一种实用的替代方案。

Method: 提出FSD-RM范式，采用容量控制的表示学习，使用CNN1D、LSTM、GRU、Transformer等已有编码器架构，对多变量遥测数据进行无监督训练，并集成到两阶段的下游寿命预测流程中。通过维度感知神经架构搜索（NAS）联合优化模型容量和输入维度。

Result: 在低温冷却器遥测数据上的实验表明，所提方法在实现竞争性预测性能的同时，降低了训练成本和模型复杂度。结果表明，在适当的归纳偏置和容量控制下，无需大规模预训练也能实现有效的表示学习。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于将成熟的表示学习技术与NAS驱动的框架相结合，针对小数据场景明确定义了参数设置和设计选择，证明了小数据条件下表示学习的可行性。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained time-series models achieve strong results through large-scale pretraining and task-agnostic representation learning, but they rely on abundant, diverse data that industrial and scientific domains often lack. We therefore propose the FSD-RM (Family of Small-Data Representation Models) paradigm as a practical alternative for limited, domain-specific telemetry. Rather than relying on large-scale pretraining, we focus on capacity-controlled representation learning using established encoder architectures (CNN1D, LSTM, GRU, Transformer), selected for their suitability in small-data settings and interpretability.
  These encoders are trained unsupervised on multivariate telemetry data and integrated into a two-stage pipeline for downstream lifetime prediction. To systematically examine architectural trade-offs under data constraints, we employ \textbf{dimension-aware neural architecture search (NAS)} to jointly optimize model capacity and input dimensionality.
  Experiments on cryocooler telemetry show that the proposed approach achieves competitive predictive performance while reducing training cost and model complexity. The contribution lies in combining established representation learning techniques within a coherent, NAS-driven framework tailored to small-data regimes, with explicitly defined parameter settings and design choices. The results indicate that effective representation learning can be achieved without large-scale pretraining when appropriate inductive bias and capacity control are applied.

</details>


### [178] [Every Cache Entry Earns Its Place: Global Allocation of Resolution and Coverage for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07001)
*Haolin Tian,Yuzhe Liu,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraceKV提出了一种全局KV缓存压缩方法，将压缩过程形式化为固定缓存预算下的全局资源分配问题，通过构建原型树实现层、头、槽之间缓存资源的自由流动和分辨率与覆盖度的联合平衡，无需额外训练且全流程在GPU上执行，在32个设置中24个排名第一，支持128倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM处理越来越长的上下文，KV缓存存储和重复访问成为主要瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖预定义的固定压缩规则，且通常单独围绕token驱逐或合并展开，导致缓存资源无法在层、头和上下文槽之间自由流动，也无法联合分配以平衡局部分辨率和信息覆盖度。

Method: GraceKV将每个层-KV头-槽组合视为原子单元，构建原型树：叶节点对应token级KV条目，内部节点用单一原型压缩其子节点覆盖的KV空间；非重叠节点集合构成原子单元的表示。新增树的根节点扩展信息覆盖度，分裂选中节点提升局部分辨率。所有候选动作为共享缓存预算全局竞争，最终保留的节点构成压缩后的KV缓存。该过程自适应决定原子单元间的缓存资源分配以及分辨率与覆盖度之间的平衡，无需额外训练，压缩和推理全程在GPU上进行。

Result: 在多种长上下文任务和压缩比下的系统实验中，GraceKV在32个设置中24个排名第一，并能在高达128倍压缩下保持鲁棒性，验证了全局预算分配在协调信息覆盖度和局部分辨率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: GraceKV通过将KV缓存压缩建模为全局资源分配问题，实现了跨层、头和槽位的缓存资源自由流动以及分辨率与覆盖度的联合平衡，在无需训练的情况下取得了显著的压缩性能，验证了全局预算分配策略的有效性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) process increasingly long contexts, KV cache storage and repeated access have become a major bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods rely on predefined, fixed compression rules and are typically developed around either token eviction or merging. As a result, cache resources can neither flow freely across layers, heads, and context slots, nor be jointly allocated to balance local resolution and information coverage. Therefore, we propose GraceKV, a global approach for the allocation of resolution and coverage in KV cache compression, and formulate the compression process as a global resource allocation problem under a fixed cache budget. GraceKV treats each layer-KV head-slot combination as an atomic unit and builds a prototype tree. Leaf nodes correspond to token-level KV entries, while each internal node uses a single prototype to compress the KV space covered by its children. A set of non-overlapping nodes in the tree forms the representation of an atomic unit. Adding the root of a new tree expands information coverage, whereas splitting a selected node improves local resolution. All candidate actions compete globally for a shared cache budget. Finally, the nodes retained across all trees form the compressed KV cache. This process adaptively determines the allocation of cache resources among atomic units globally and the balance between resolution and coverage. GraceKV requires no additional training, and the entire compression and inference process is performed on the GPU. Systematic experiments across diverse long-context tasks and compression ratios show that GraceKV ranks first in 24 of 32 settings and remains robust up to 128-fold compression. These results validate the effectiveness of global budget allocation in coordinating information coverage and local resolution.

</details>


### [179] [Hyperbolic Graph Embedders for Link Prediction and Topology Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07029)
*Robert Jankowski,Maksim Kitsak,Dorota Celińska-Kopczyńska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一协议下系统比较了13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法，涵盖链接预测与拓扑重建任务，发现最大似然方法和表示学习方法整体表现最优，但无单一方法在所有任务和结构区间占优，性能与嵌入范式而非学科来源更相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲嵌入为复杂网络提供紧凑的几何表示，但来自机器学习、网络科学和算法学等多个学科的方法缺乏系统性对比研究，导致方法选择缺乏实证依据。

Method: 采用统一的基准测试协议，对13种无监督双曲图嵌入方法在合成与经验网络上进行链接预测和拓扑重建评估，协议同时考察缺失链接恢复能力以及局部和全局网络结构的保持情况。

Result: 最大似然方法和基于表示学习方法（含混合变体）整体性能最强，但没有方法在所有任务和结构区间中占据主导地位；性能主要受嵌入范式影响而非学科来源。

Conclusion: 识别了不同范式在不同网络结构区间中的优劣表现，为下游应用中的方法选择提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Hyperbolic embeddings provide compact geometric representations of complex networks in hyperbolic spaces, but systematic comparisons of methods developed in machine learning, network science, and algorithmics remain rare. We benchmark 13 unsupervised hyperbolic graph embedders under a unified protocol for link prediction and topology reconstruction on synthetic and empirical networks. The protocol captures both missing-link recovery and the preservation of local and global network structure. Maximum-likelihood and representation-learning-based approaches, including hybrid variants, achieve the strongest overall performance, although no method dominates across all tasks and structural regimes. Performance is more strongly associated with embedding paradigm than with disciplinary origin. We identify the network regimes in which different paradigms succeed or fail and provide practical guidance for method selection in downstream applications.

</details>


### [180] [Accounting Graph Transformer for Short-History Multi-KPI Forecasting in Small Businesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07037)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出会计图变换器（AGT），利用会计关系图结构，从71个月度账目序列联合预测13个跨三张报表的KPI的12个月值。在1060家未见公司的11993个预测起点上，AGT达到0.6990的样本加权KPI宏平均MAE，显著优于LightGBM基线（0.7378），并在全部13个KPI上优于LightGBM、TimeMixer和SOFTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型企业通常只有12-24个月的会计历史，但规划与风险管理工作流需要跨财务报表的协调预测。现有方法未能充分利用会计科目之间的结构化关系（如资产负债表、利润表、现金流量表之间的勾稽关系），导致跨报表、多KPI的联合预测精度不足。

Method: AGT将每条账目序列表示为掩码令牌，通过基于固定会计关系图的类型化注意力机制交换信息，池化目标特定上下文，并将其与门控的三个月近期路径融合。模型采用5.3M参数的单一模型，无需针对公司进行特定拟合即可生成156个对齐的预测。

Result: 在1060家未见公司上，AGT的MAE为0.6990±0.0013，优于LightGBM的0.7378±0.0014；种子42匹配对比中，AGT在全部13个KPI上均优于LightGBM、TimeMixer和SOFTS；消融实验验证了关系注意力、会计拓扑和近期路径各自的有效性；在2025年1-5月的7094家额外未见公司上，AGT仍优于SOFTS。

Conclusion: 结合会计领域结构化知识（会计关系图）的神经架构能够显著提升小型企业跨财务报表的多KPI联合预测精度，为集成规划、流动性和营运资本分析提供了统一的预测层。

Abstract: Small businesses often have only 12-24 months of accounting history, yet planning and risk workflows require coordinated forecasts across financial statements. We study joint 12-month forecasting of 13 income-statement, balance-sheet, cash-flow, and working-capital key performance indicators (KPIs) from 71 monthly ledger series. We introduce the Accounting Graph Transformer (AGT), which represents each ledger series as a masked token, exchanges information through typed attention on a fixed accounting-relation graph, pools target-specific context, and fuses it with a gated three-month recency path. Across 11,993 forecast origins from 1,060 unseen companies, AGT achieves sample-weighted KPI-macro mean absolute error (MAE) $0.6990 \pm 0.0013$ over three independent seeds, compared with $0.7378 \pm 0.0014$ for the strongest baseline, LightGBM. At the pre-specified seed 42, a paired company-clustered bootstrap gives a LightGBM-minus-AGT difference of 0.0395 with 95% confidence interval (CI) $[0.0350,0.0439]$. AGT is best on all 13 KPIs against LightGBM, TimeMixer, and SOFTS in the matched seed-42 comparison, while final-architecture ablations show that relational attention, accounting topology, and the recency path each improve validation and test accuracy. On 7,094 additional unseen companies with origins sampled from January-May 2025, AGT obtains 0.7548 MAE versus 0.7694 for SOFTS. A single 5.3M-parameter model produces 156 aligned forecasts without company-specific fitting, providing one forecasting layer for integrated planning, liquidity, and working-capital analysis.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Isolation: Unlocking Reinforcement Learning Component Synergy for Sample-Efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07086)
*Qi Zhao,Guozheng Ma,Yilun Kong,Lu Li,Haoyu Wang,Zilin Wang,Tiantian Zhang,Yuxing Wang,Jian Sha,Yongzhe Chang,Xueqian Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化学习系统中不同算法组件之间的功能依赖关系，发现各组件的有效性高度依赖任务，盲目堆叠SOTA技术反而会引发复合非平稳性等新问题。基于这些发现，作者提出ROSER框架，协调基于模型的表征、优化稳定性和经验回放三个关键维度，在连续控制基准上比朴素堆叠方案提升17.60%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习系统因固有特性远比其他机器学习范式复杂，其系统设计需要同时考虑多个紧密耦合的因素。尽管各算法组件在独立层面取得了进展，但组件间的功能依赖关系尚未被充分探索——它们究竟是相互协同还是相互干扰仍是开放问题，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 本文首先进行系统性实证研究，揭示不同RL组件的有效性存在显著的任务依赖性，并发现朴素堆叠SOTA技术往往引发负面效应（如复合非平稳性）。基于这些洞察，提出ROSER框架，从三个关键维度进行协调：基于模型的表征（Model-based Representation）、优化稳定性（Optimization Stability）和经验回放（Experience Replay）。

Result: 在多种连续控制基准上，ROSER始终优于普通基线，相比朴素堆叠方案取得17.60%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在RL系统设计中采用整体性视角的必要性，为开发样本高效的智能体铺平了道路，并指出组件间的协同协调远比单纯堆叠先进技术更为重要。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning systems are significantly more complex than other machine learning paradigms due to inherent properties, causing RL system design to jointly account for many tightly coupled factors. Despite advances in individual algorithmic components, their functional interdependencies remain underexplored: do they exhibit mutual synergy or counterproductive interference? To bridge this gap, we conduct a systematic investigation and find that the efficacy of different components exhibits significant task-dependency, and naively stacking state-of-the-art techniques does not necessarily yield performance gains; instead, it often triggers emergent challenges, such as compounded non-stationarity. Building upon these findings, we distill a suite of actionable insights into the principled coordination of these components. Guided by these insights, we propose ROSER, an RL framework that coordinates three critical dimensions: Model-based Representation, Optimization Stability, and Experience Replay. Across diverse continuous-control benchmarks, ROSER consistently outperforms vanilla baselines and achieves 17.60% gains over naive stack. Our findings underscore the necessity of a holistic perspective in RL system design and paves the way for developing sample-efficient agents.

</details>


### [182] [Modular TTT: Rethinking Test-Time Training as Composable Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07110)
*Bohao Tang,Zhen Qin,Yuqi Pan,Zheng Li,Pengfei Liu,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Modular TTT框架，将测试时训练（TTT）的内部学习器表示为有向无环图，通过模块化组合自动生成完整的TTT计算流程，并利用该系统系统性地消融各组件，最终训练出与Gated DeltaNet性能相当的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TTT变体往往各自硬编码实现，导致设计新TTT方法困难，且难以隔离和评估每个组件（如快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减、归一化等）的具体作用。

Method: 提出Modular TTT框架，将内部学习器表示为有向无环图（DAG），将快速权重网络、损失函数、学习率、权重衰减和归一化作为显式设计维度。该框架自动将原语级别的训练视图前向、训练视图反向和因果查询视图规则组合为完整的图级别TTT计算（包括快速权重状态转换）。利用该框架对TTT各组件进行系统性消融实验。

Result: 发现小的学习率初始化、权重衰减和单层非线性能提升性能；MSE与内积损失表现相似；更深的快速权重网络和归一化因导致过大的激活值而损害性能；残差连接和门控几乎没有可衡量的收益。依据这些发现训练的410M和1.45B参数模型在100B tokens上训练的损失和基准性能与Gated DeltaNet相当。

Conclusion: Modular TTT提供了一个灵活的、可组合的框架来研究和设计TTT方法，通过系统性消融揭示了各组件对TTT性能的影响，并验证了据此优化后的模型能达到与既有强基线相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) views sequence modeling as an online learning problem in which fast weights are updated by an internal learning rule. Despite the growing number of TTT variants, existing approaches typically hard-code each variant separately, which makes it difficult to design new TTT methods and to isolate the role of each component. To address this, we propose Modular TTT, a framework that represents the inner learner as a directed acyclic graph and exposes the fast-weight network, loss function, learning rate, weight decay, and normalization as explicit design dimensions. Modular TTT automatically composes primitive-level train-view forward, train-view backward, and causal query-view rules into the full graph-level TTT computation, including the fast-weight state transition. Using Modular TTT, we systematically ablate the components of TTT and find that small learning-rate initialization, weight decay, and a single-layer nonlinearity improve performance, while MSE and inner-product losses perform similarly. Deeper fast-weight networks and normalization tend to hurt performance because they induce excessively large activations, while residual connections and gating provide little measurable benefit. Guided by these findings, we train the best resulting variant as 410M- and 1.45B-parameter models on 100B tokens, and observe training loss and benchmark performance comparable to Gated DeltaNet.

</details>


### [183] [Synthetic LiDAR Data Generation and Deterministic Downsampling for Point Cloud Classification on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07106)
*Niclas Meyer,Stefan Reitmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Raspberry Pi 5的低功耗嵌入式3D深度学习推理工作流，通过物理仿真生成合成LiDAR数据集解决清洁CAD数据与真实传感器数据的领域差距，并集成特征驱动的Critical Points Layer（CPL）前端过滤器替代传统的距离排序预处理。在ARM Cortex-A76处理器上实现约50 FPS的推理吞吐量和88.36%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维深度学习框架在低功耗嵌入式处理器上的部署受限于空间数据的非结构化特性以及神经网络推理前耗资源的距离排序算法。此外，清洁CAD数据集与真实传感器数据之间存在领域差距（reality gap），导致网络在实际场景中性能显著下降。

Method: 1) 使用基于物理的仿真构建合成LiDAR数据集，以模拟真实传感器噪声；2) 将特征驱动的Critical Points Layer（CPL）作为前端过滤器集成到流程中，用于确定性压缩原始点云（从1024个点压缩至40-60个唯一坐标）；3) 在Raspberry Pi 5的ARM Cortex-A76处理器上进行硬件约束下的性能分析和优化。

Result: 跨数据集评估显示在清洁CAD数据上训练的网络在合成LiDAR数据上准确率大幅下降，验证了传感器感知训练的必要性。集成CPL后，完整流水线在ARM Cortex-A76上实现约50 FPS的推理吞吐量，实例分类准确率达88.36%，证明了确定性实时3D边缘感知的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过传感器感知训练和特征驱动的前端过滤策略，可以在低功耗边缘设备上实现确定性的实时3D感知，为嵌入式3D深度学习的实际部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Deploying three-dimensional deep learning frameworks to low-power embedded processors is bottlenecked by the unstructured nature of spatial data and the resource-intensive distance sorting algorithms often used before neural network inference. To address this gap, this paper presents a hardware-constrained workflow optimized for native execution on the Raspberry Pi 5. To account for the reality gap between noiseless, clean computer-aided design (CAD) datasets and real-world sensor data, we use physics-based simulation to construct a synthetic LiDAR dataset. Cross-dataset evaluations demonstrate a substantial drop in classification accuracy when networks trained on clean CAD data are evaluated on synthetic LiDAR sensor data, highlighting the critical need for sensor-aware training. To address the latency bottleneck of traditional geometric preprocessing on edge CPUs, we integrate an isolated, feature-driven Critical Points Layer (CPL) as a frontend filter. Our results show that the pretrained CPL deterministically compresses raw 1024-point clouds to a subset of 40 to 60 unique coordinates. When profiled on the ARM Cortex-A76 processor, the complete pipeline achieves an inference throughput of approximately 50 FPS while maintaining an instance classification accuracy of 88.36%, demonstrating the viability of deterministic real-time 3D perception at the edge.

</details>


### [184] [Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAL框架，一种轨迹相对的后见之明蒸馏方法，通过统一的回合对齐评分协议在不同决策回合间重新分配密集监督信号。该方法在WebShop和ALFWorld上全面超越GRPO，并在六项评估中取得最佳或并列最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体强化学习方法利用后见之明来补充稀疏的结果奖励，但一条完整的轨迹可以产生许多这样的信号，如何在不同回合之间合理分配这些监督信号成为一个未解决的问题。过度或不足分配监督信号都会影响学习效率。

Method: TRIAL框架的核心包括：(1) 对每个决策回合提取该决策实际后果的结果视图；(2) 在普通上下文和后见之明条件下评估同一响应；(3) 使用符号化的对数概率差距确定token级监督的方向和局部强度；(4) 通过轨迹内部的联合归一化来确定回合级分配幅度，使合格token加权均值恒为1，从而在不改变平均乘数的情况下重新分配密集监督。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld上的八种骨干网络、环境和评估指标组合中，TRIAL全面优于GRPO。在WebShop上使用Qwen3-1.7B时，成功率从56.4%提升至75.2%，任务得分从78.7%提升至85.7%。在六种方法中，TRIAL在六项评估上取得最佳或并列最佳表现。消融实验表明，轨迹相对的回合分配相比单纯的密集后见之明蒸馏能带来显著额外收益。

Conclusion: TRAIL验证了轨迹相对的后见之明监督分配范式，表明通过统一的回合对齐评分协议和轨迹内联合归一化，可以更有效地分配密集监督信号，从而显著提升智能体强化学习的性能。回合级分配机制是超越单纯密集蒸馏的关键因素。

Abstract: Recent agentic reinforcement learning methods use hindsight to complement sparse outcome rewards. However, a completed rollout can yield many such signals, leaving their appropriate allocation across turns unclear. We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditioned contexts. The signed log-probability gap determines the direction and local strength of token-level supervision, while turn-level magnitudes are normalized jointly over the realized trajectory. The resulting allocation multipliers have an eligible-token-weighted mean of one, redistributing dense supervision across turns while fixing its average multiplier. Experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld with different backbones show that TRIAL outperforms GRPO across all eight combinations of backbone, environment, and evaluation metric, while achieving the best or tied-best performance among six methods on six of them. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, TRIAL improves the success rate from 56.4% to 75.2% and the task score from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations further show that trajectory-relative turn allocation provides substantial gains beyond those of dense hindsight distillation alone.

</details>


### [185] [Online Conformal Prediction Beyond Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07139)
*Joar Skalse,Edoardo Pona,Osvaldo Simeone,Nicola Paoletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OCPQ，一种在无反馈设置下的在线符合预测方法。在每轮中，学习者要么输出预测集要么查询真实标签（不能同时进行），通过将问题转化为部分监测博弈，利用标签高效预测器（label efficient forecaster）实现O(T^{2/3})的期望遗憾和至少β-O(T^{-1/3})的期望覆盖率，仅需查询T^{-1/3}比例的轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线符合预测（OCP）通常依赖先前部署预测的反馈来更新预测集，但在许多实际场景中，部署的预测无法被直接评估（即无反馈环境）。作者希望探索超越反馈的OCP设置，在预测与查询只能二选一的约束下，仍能提供理论上有保证的不确定性量化。

Method: 将问题归约到部分监测博弈（partial monitoring game），其中预测动作不产生观察，单独的查询动作揭示真实标签。构造鼓励输出小预测集且保证覆盖率充分的奖励函数。通过改编Cesa-Bianchi等人（2004）的标签高效预测器（label efficient forecaster）来求解该博弈，实现在线符合预测与查询的联合优化。

Result: 对于任意黑盒分类器和任意（非独立同分布）遗忘型数据流（长度T），OCPQ达到O(T^{2/3})的期望遗憾，期望覆盖率至少为β-O(T^{-1/3})（β为用户指定参数），且仅在T^{-1/3}比例的轮次查询标签。实验在真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OCPQ在无反馈的在线符合预测场景中实现了与基于bandit的OCP方法相当的覆盖率保证，同时避免了部署预测集的反馈需求，仅以较小的查询成本换取理论保证。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is essential when deploying machine learning models in safety-critical applications. Online conformal prediction (OCP) provides theoretically principled uncertainty quantification for arbitrary black-box classifiers and non-i.i.d. data streams by constructing prediction sets that are guaranteed to contain the true label at a user-specified frequency. OCP usually updates prediction sets using feedback from previously deployed predictions. We instead study an OCP setting beyond feedback: on each round, the learner can either output a prediction set or query the correct label, but not both. Thus, no deployed prediction is ever evaluated directly. We reduce this problem to a partial monitoring game in which prediction actions return no observation and a separate query action reveals the label. The reward function is constructed in a way that encourages the learner to output small prediction sets while ensuring that the correct label is covered with a sufficiently high probability. To solve this game, we develop OCP with queries (OCPQ) by adapting the label efficient forecaster of Cesa-Bianchi, Lugosi, and Stoltz (2004) to our setting. For any black box classifier and any (non-i.i.d.) oblivious data stream of length $T$, OCPQ has $O(T^{2/3})$ expected regret and expected coverage at least $β-O(T^{-1/3})$ for a user-defined $β$, while querying only an expected $T^{-1/3}$ fraction of rounds. This provides coverage comparable to bandit-based OCP methods while requiring no feedback from deployed prediction sets. Experiments on real-world datasets further demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [186] [Interpretable reinforcement learning with decision-tree pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07151)
*Mark Leon Ringer,Michel Tokic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对基于决策树规则策略的剪枝流程，通过结构化和使用感知的操作符，在保持任务性能的同时简化策略结构、提升可解释性，并使策略编辑可审计。在经典控制和MuJoCo基准上验证了一致的可解释性提升和性能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略难以被人类审查，而将训练好的策略转换为决策树规则虽然提升了透明度，但生成的规则往往仍过于复杂，难以被人类理解。因此需要一种既能简化规则策略、又能保持性能且可审计的剪枝方法。

Method: 该方法定义了一小组结构化和使用感知的剪枝操作符，通过重新执行策略来评估候选剪枝操作的效果，使用回报（return）和可解释性代理指标作为评估标准，迭代地将复杂规则策略转换为紧凑的策略结构。

Result: 在经典控制（classic control）和MuJoCo基准任务上进行实验，剪枝轨迹（pruning traces）显示出一致的可解释性提升，同时保持较高的任务性能。

Conclusion: 该剪枝方法能够有效简化决策树形式的强化学习策略，实现性能与可解释性的平衡，且由于每次编辑都通过重新执行来评估，整个过程具备可审计性，有助于提升强化学习系统的可信度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning policies are difficult to inspect, but interpreting them is a prerequisite for trustworthiness. Converting a trained policy into explicit decision-tree rules improves transparency and the resulting artifacts often remain too complex for human understanding. We present a pruning process that simplifies such rule-based policies while preserving task performance and making edits to the policy auditable. The process defines a small set of structural and usage-aware operators and evaluates candidate edits by re-executing the policy to measure return and interpretability proxies. This exposes an transformation process from complex to compact policy structures. We investigate this approach on classic control and MuJoCo benchmarks, where pruning traces reveal consistent interpretability improvements while maintaining high performance.

</details>


### [187] [Machine Learning-Based Inter-Crystal Scatter Recovery for Ultra-High Resolution PET Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07155)
*Alexandre Bernier,Roger Lecomte,Jean-Baptiste Michaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种前馈神经网络方法来恢复超高分辨率PET中的晶体间散射（ICS）事件，通过推断首次康普顿相互作用的响应线（LOR），在将灵敏度提升70%至106%的同时保持亚毫米级的空间分辨率（最低1.6 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超高分辨率PET（UHR-PET）中，随着探测器晶体尺寸减小和读出通道细分，晶体间散射（ICS）事件成为一个显著挑战。传统方法要么拒绝这些事件导致灵敏度下降，要么接受它们但使用次优的定位算法导致图像分辨率退化。亟需一种能同时兼顾灵敏度和分辨率的ICS事件恢复方法。

Method: 作者设计了一个前馈神经网络（feed forward neural network），用于推断首次康普顿相互作用所归属的响应线（line-of-response, LOR），从而优化ICS事件的恢复。该方法的验证基于蒙特卡洛模拟以及来自全像素化的LabPET-II临床前和脑UHR-PET扫描仪的实验数据。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统方法相比，该方法在保持亚毫米空间可分辨性（低至1.6 mm）的同时，将灵敏度提高了70%至106%。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种有效的ICS恢复方案，可补偿UHR-PET中小的像素化探测器所带来的较低检测效率，从而在基本保持图像质量的前提下支持缩短扫描时间和降低辐射剂量。

Abstract: Inter-crystal scatter (ICS) events pose a significant challenge in ultrahigh- resolution positron emission tomography (UHR-PET), especially as detector crystals become smaller and their readouts increasingly segmented. Current approaches either reject these events, reducing sensitivity, or accept them with suboptimal positioning algorithms, degrading image resolution. We present a feed forward neural network to optimize ICS event recovery by inferring the line-of-response belonging to the first Compton interaction. Our approach was validated using both Monte Carlo simulations and experimental data from the fully pixelated LabPET-IIbased preclinical and brain UHR-PET scanners.Results demonstrate a 70% to 106% increase in sensitivity while preserving sub-millimeter spatial resolvability (down to 1.6 mm) compared to conventional methods. This ICS recovery approach is an effective solution that compensates for the lower detection efficiency of small, pixelated detectors in UHR-PET, enabling reduced scan times and lower radiation doses while largely preserving image quality.

</details>


### [188] [Capacity Confounds and Coverage Guarantees in Adaptive Sub-model Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07157)
*Alireza Moayedikia,Alicia Troncoso Lora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究子模型联邦学习中的自适应容量分配问题。以HAS-FL为测试用例，发现：1)更新散度对数据异质性的估计被设备容量所主导而非数据本身，存在此前未记录的混杂偏差；2)自适应分配存在隐藏故障模式——当所有客户端低于全宽度时，未覆盖参数停留在随机初始化状态并逐步腐蚀全局模型；3)在匹配预算下，自适应分配并不优于随机分配，甚至在某些数据集上表现最差。子模型训练的价值在于参数覆盖率而非分配智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模型联邦学习按设备资源分配容量，而根据服务器已观察到的更新来估计各客户端数据异质性的自适应容量分配方法被反复建议，但这一步骤是否可行尚未得到验证。

Method: 以HAS-FL作为自适应容量分配框架的测试用例，在可复现的分区上使用ground-truth标签分布散度进行验证，对比两种修正估计器；分析自适应分配在客户端被限制在低于全宽度时的故障模式；通过匹配预算控制实验比较自适应、随机和均匀三种分配策略。

Result: 1)更新散度估计与设备容量呈强烈的负相关，控制容量后数据信号消失；2)当所有客户端受限于全宽度以下时，未覆盖参数保持随机初始化并损坏全局模型，覆盖率保证可消除该故障并能解释均匀分配的崩溃；3)匹配预算下，随机分配与自适应分配在图像基准上表现无差异，在文本基准上自适应策略性能最差且消耗最多容量。

Conclusion: 子模型训练因以二次方降低的成本接纳受限客户端而有价值，但保护精度的关键是参数覆盖率而非分配智能。其表面收益来自容量预算和覆盖率，未来设计需要能区分容量效应的异质性信号。

Abstract: Sub-model federated learning lets resource-constrained clients train width-reduced versions of a global model, but existing methods allocate capacity by device resources alone. A natural next step, allocating capacity by each client's data heterogeneity as estimated from the updates the server already observes, has been repeatedly suggested. We ask whether that step is possible, using HAS-FL, an adaptive capacity-allocation framework, as a test case. Our findings are threefold. First, validated against ground-truth label-distribution divergence on reproducible partitions, update-divergence estimates of client heterogeneity are dominated by capacity rather than data: across two corrected estimators, multiple datasets, and all seeds, the estimates correlate strongly and negatively with device capacity, and no data signal remains once capacity is controlled for. This previously undocumented confound affects any method estimating client statistics from sub-model updates. Second, adaptive allocation has a hidden failure mode: when every client is capped below full width, the uncovered parameters stay at random initialization and progressively corrupt the global model. A simple coverage guarantee removes the failure and explains why uniform allocation collapses. Third, a matched-budget control settles what adaptivity contributes: random allocation to the same average budget performs no differently on both image benchmarks, and on the naturally partitioned text benchmark the adaptive policy is the weakest of the three strategies while consuming the most capacity. Sub-model training remains valuable because it admits constrained clients at quadratically reduced cost, but what protects accuracy is parameter coverage rather than allocation intelligence. Its apparent benefits come from capacity budgeting and coverage, and future designs need heterogeneity signals separable from capacity effects.

</details>


### [189] [Edge Sparsification via Temporal Forman-Ricci Curvature for Dynamic Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07158)
*Poupak Azad,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Kiarash Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRicci框架，基于网络曲率（Forman-Ricci曲率）对大规模时序图进行边稀疏化，在保持预测性能的前提下压缩约80%的图结构，并将端到端训练和推理时间平均减少55.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模时序图学习在网络密集且快速变化时计算开销极大，现有方法难以在保持预测性能的同时实现高效训练，亟需一种可扩展的稀疏化方案来降低计算成本。

Method: 将经典的Forman-Ricci曲率扩展到有向加权时序图（TRicci），通过捕捉结构支撑、时序最近性和局部交互竞争三个维度来评估边的预测性重要度，以此为基础对时序图进行边稀疏化。

Result: 在9个交易网络和3个时序图基准数据集上，TRicci将时序图稀疏化约80%，端到端下游训练和推理时间平均降低55.94%，且预测性能无明显下降。

Conclusion: 时序曲率可作为大规模时序图学习的理论基础，在显著稀疏化下仍能保留预测性的时序-结构信息，为实现可扩展的时序图学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning has become essential for analyzing real-world systems whose interactions continuously evolve over time, including financial transaction networks, communication systems, and online social platforms. However, learning from large-scale temporal graphs remains computationally challenging when networks are dense and rapidly changing. To address this limitation, we propose a network-curvature-inspired edge sparsification framework for dynamic graph learning. Our proposed method, TRicci, extends classical Forman-Ricci curvature to directed weighted temporal graphs by capturing structural support, temporal recency, and local interaction competition.
  Experiments on 9 transaction networks and 3 temporal graph benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework preserves predictive performance across multiple graph-level prediction tasks. The results show that TRicci sparsifies temporal graphs by approximately 80% while reducing end-to-end downstream training and inference time by an average of 55.94%, without substantial degradation in predictive performance. Our findings suggest that temporal curvature can serve as a principled basis for scalable temporal graph learning by preserving predictive temporal-structural information under substantial sparsification.

</details>


### [190] [Fluid-DiT: Graph-Free Diffusion Transformers for Fluid Flow Simulations Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07161)
*Shentong Mo,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fluid-DiT，一种无图扩散Transformer，用基于注意力机制的去噪取代图消息传递，在隐空间中进行扩散学习，解决了扩散图网络在复杂流体模拟中受限于手工架构、消息传递感受野有限和多尺度设计成本高等问题。在多个基准上（层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流、湍流3D机翼）优于图基扩散基线，并展示了从短轨迹到未知雷诺数和几何体的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真流体模拟需要捕获完整的平衡分布而非仅平均轨迹，但计算代价极高。已有DGN虽结合扩散模型与图神经网络在非结构网格上直接采样平衡态，但受限于手工设计的图架构约束、消息传递的有限感受野以及多尺度设计的高成本，难以扩展到更大更复杂的域。

Method: Fluid-DiT采用无图的扩散Transformer框架：1）用基于注意力机制的去噪过程替代图消息传递，消除显式图设计；2）引入隐空间公式，将几何保真与分布学习解耦，减少高频伪影并加速采样；3）利用Transformer的全局感受野，不依赖层级图粗化即可同时捕捉局部流动结构和长程关联。

Result: 在层流圆柱尾流、椭圆流系统和湍流3D机翼等基准上，Fluid-DiT在样本质量和分布精度上均一致超越图基扩散基线，取得更高R²相关性和更低Wasserstein距离。此外，从短且不完整的轨迹到未见雷诺数和几何体的泛化表现稳健，展示了强可扩展性。

Conclusion: Fluid-DiT通过无图扩散Transformer架构有效解决了图基扩散方法在可扩展性和架构约束上的局限，在分布精度、泛化能力和计算效率方面均有显著提升，为复杂流体平衡态分布模拟提供了更具可扩展性的新方向。

Abstract: Simulating complex fluid flows requires capturing full equilibrium distributions rather than just mean trajectories, yet high-fidelity solvers remain computationally prohibitive. Recent advances, such as Diffusion Graph Networks (DGNs), have combined diffusion models with graph neural networks to sample equilibrium states directly from unstructured meshes, enabling distributional accuracy even from short simulations. However, graph-based diffusion approaches suffer from hand-crafted architectural constraints, limited receptive fields in message passing, and costly multi-scale designs, which restrict scalability to larger and more complex domains. We propose Fluid-DiT, a Graph-Free Diffusion Transformer that replaces graph message passing with attention-based denoising, eliminating explicit graph design while preserving the ability to model distributions of chaotic flows. Our framework introduces a latent-space formulation that disentangles geometric fidelity from distributional learning, reducing high-frequency artifacts and accelerating sampling. By leveraging the transformer's global receptive field, Fluid-DiT naturally captures both local flow structures and long-range correlations without requiring hierarchical graph coarsening. On canonical benchmarks including laminar cylinder wakes, ellipse-flow systems, and turbulent 3D wing experiments, Fluid-DiT consistently outperforms graph-based diffusion baselines in both sample quality and distributional accuracy, achieving higher $R^2$ correlations and lower Wasserstein distances. Moreover, it generalizes robustly from short, incomplete trajectories to unseen Reynolds numbers and geometries, demonstrating strong scalability.

</details>


### [191] [Momba: Network Modernization Improves Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07180)
*Adam Štafa,Santeri Heiskanen,Petr Novotný,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将深度强化学习的神经网络架构改进（如观测/特征归一化、权重归一化和分布回报建模）应用于多目标强化学习（MORL）算法中，以提升解集质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去MORL的研究主要聚焦于算法创新而忽略了网络架构的改进。MORL在不同权衡下的最优策略和价值函数差异显著，但常用简单的以权衡为条件的前馈网络表示，作者质疑能否通过更具表达力的函数逼近器提升算法性能。

Method: 将三类神经网络设计进展融入一个熵正则化的MORL算法中：(i) 观测与特征归一化，(ii) 权重归一化，(iii) 分布回报建模。这些改动无需对底层算法进行大幅修改。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准上，这些改动在不修改底层算法的情况下，显著提升了生成解集的质量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，架构层面的改进同样能有效增强MORL算法的性能，为MORL中网络架构的进一步探索提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep reinforcement learning (RL) have shown that improving neural network architectures can yield substantial gains in sample efficiency and asymptotic performance without altering the underlying algorithms. In contrast, work on multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL), which aims to discover a set of policies that balance trade-offs among conflicting objectives, has predominantly focused on algorithmic innovations, leaving the area of architectures underexplored. While the optimal policies and value functions can differ significantly depending on the trade-offs, MORL algorithms commonly represent them with simple feedforward networks conditioned on the trade-off. This raises the question of whether the performance of the algorithms could be improved with more expressive function approximators. In this paper, we integrate recent advances in neural network design: (i) observation and feature normalization, (ii) weight normalization, and (iii) modeling of distributional returns with an entropy-regularized MORL algorithm. The empirical results across standard continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that these changes substantially improve the quality of the produced solution sets without requiring major changes to the underlying algorithm.

</details>


### [192] [Conformal Fusion Under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07183)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCCF（Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion），一种同时解决多模态缺失鲁棒性和不确定性校准两个耦合问题的架构。MCCF结合模态丢弃训练的瓶颈融合骨干、逐模态证据头产生的Dirichlet分布、Dempster-Shafer融合规则以及基于模态存在掩码的Mondrian共形校准模块，首次通过架构集成而非事后重校准，在任意模态可用性下提供形式化的组条件覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现存多模态融合架构假设推理时所有模态可用，但传感器故障、采集变异和成本约束导致观测不完整。现有工作仅将模态缺失视为预测精度问题，而忽略了一个更基本的问题：当整个输入流被移除时，模型的置信度估计是否仍然校准。作者主张缺失模态鲁棒性与校准不确定性是一个耦合属性，应当一并解决。

Method: MCCF包含四个核心组件：(1) 模态瓶颈融合骨干，训练时使用模态丢弃；(2) 逐模态证据头，产生模态分解的Dirichlet分布；(3) Dempster-Shafer组合规则，将逐模态证据融合为联合预测分布，缺失模态贡献空泛证据被结构化忽略；(4) Mondrian共形校准模块，以模态存在掩码为键，为每个非空模态子集提供有限样本组条件覆盖保证。无需测试时插补。

Result: 在合成问题及三个真实多模态基准上，MCCF在每一个模态存在子集上均保持目标覆盖水平，相比边际分割共形基线显著缩小了完整与部分模态之间的覆盖差距，且相对温度缩放和证据基线不引入可测量的精度损失。逐模态空泛分数可定位不确定性来源至导致缺失的模态。

Conclusion: MCCF是首个通过架构集成而非事后重校准、在任意模态可用性下提供形式化覆盖保证的方法，并证明了模态缺失鲁棒性与不确定性校准可以作为一个统一问题被优雅地解决。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion architectures typically assume all modalities are available at inference, yet sensor failures, acquisition variability, and cost constraints routinely produce incomplete observations. Existing work treats modality absence as a prediction-accuracy problem, leaving a more basic question unanswered: whether a model's confidence estimates remain calibrated when an entire input stream is removed. We argue that missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty are a single coupled property, and introduce Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion (MCCF), an architecture that addresses both at once. MCCF combines a multimodal bottleneck fusion backbone trained with modality dropout, per-modality evidential heads producing modality-decomposed Dirichlet distributions, and a Dempster-Shafer combination rule that fuses the per-modality evidence into a joint predictive distribution; an absent modality contributes vacuous evidence that is structurally ignored, so the fused uncertainty automatically reflects the reduced information without test-time imputation. A Mondrian conformal calibration module keyed on the modality-presence mask then provides finite-sample group-conditional coverage for every non-empty modality subset. MCCF is, to our knowledge, the first method with formal coverage guarantees under arbitrary modality availability through architectural integration rather than post-hoc recalibration, and the evidential decomposition yields per-modality vacuity scores that localise uncertainty to the absent modality responsible. Across a synthetic problem and three real multimodal benchmarks, MCCF holds its target coverage on every modality-presence subset, substantially narrows the coverage gap between full and partial modalities relative to a marginal split-conformal baseline, and imposes no measurable accuracy cost relative to temperature-scaled and evidential baselines.

</details>


### [193] [MAUPITI: On-Device Prototype-Based Learning on a Smart Infrared Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07192)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Tanguy Dugas du Villard,Matteo Risso,Alessio Burrello,Francesco Daghero,Enrico Macii,Massimo Poncino,Marco Castellano,Alfio Basile,Daniele Jahier Pagliari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种集成了16×16热MOSFET（TMOS）阵列和RISC-V微控制器的智能多像素红外传感器，通过原型式最近类均值（NCM）分类器实现在设备上的持续学习，在<32kB内存和约1.5mW功耗约束下完成姿态与手势识别任务，性能与传统分类器相当且更新开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率红外阵列传感器是嵌入式系统中隐私保护人体感知的理想方案，但需要在严格的存储和功耗限制下实现在线学习和持续适应，而传统反向传播和回放缓冲区方法的内存开销过大。

Method: 采用基于原型的最近类均值（NCM）分类器，CNN编码器离线训练并量化，类原型以流式方式在设备上存储和更新；RISC-V微控制器扩展了低精度SIMD指令以支持片上学习。

Result: 在两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分类精度上与传统分类器相当，分类和原型更新的延迟开销极低（两者合计<0.29%），有效实现了感知框架的在线自适应。

Conclusion: 该方法在极低存储和功耗约束下成功实现了隐私保护的红外传感器的可在线学习和持续适应能力，为嵌入式人体感知系统提供了一种高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-resolution infrared (IR) array sensors represent an interesting solution for privacy-preserving human sensing in embedded systems. In this letter, we describe a smart multi-pixel IR sensor integrating a 16$\times$16 thermal MOSFET (TMOS) array and a RISC-V microcontroller extended with low-precision SIMD instructions, capable of on-device learning and continual adaptation for pose and gesture recognition tasks under tight memory and power constraints ($<$32kB on-chip memory, $\approx$1.5mW). To avoid the memory overheads of backpropagation and replay buffers, we adopt a prototype-based Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier in which a simple Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) encoder is trained and quantized offline, while class prototypes are stored and updated on the device in streaming mode. With experiments on two datasets, we show that this approach yields accuracy on par with a conventional classifier, with negligible latency overheads in both the classification and the prototype update ($<$0.29% considering both phases), effectively enabling online adaptation of the perception framework.

</details>


### [194] [An AI4AI Framework for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07193)
*Zhen Liu,Wenli Huang,Wei Song,Yuhan Liu,Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoPrune提出了一种AI4AI（用AI设计AI）框架，通过大型语言模型自动设计视觉token剪枝策略，无需训练。其核心是一种包含131个可复用原子的Token剪枝领域特定语言（TPDSL），将搜索状态表示为对强基策略的残差修改，显著缩小搜索空间。在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上验证了效果，可移除94.4%的视觉token而保留99%以上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖固定手工启发式和昂贵的专家试错，随着剪枝目标、预算和模型架构的多样化，手工设计空间变得难以驾驭。作者希望回答一个自然问题：LLM能否自动设计有效的视觉token剪枝算法？关键难点在于如何将LLM的通用算法知识转化为针对该专门任务的有效方案。

Method: AutoPrune是一个免训练的LLM驱动视觉token剪枝策略设计框架。核心贡献是Token剪枝领域特定语言（TPDSL），包含131个可复用原子，覆盖预算控制、token评分、选择约束和token重组四个方面。TPDSL的关键特性是每个搜索状态被表示为对强基策略的残差修改，这种残差公式化设计缩小了搜索空间，并将LLM的注意力引导到对性能影响最大的策略组件上。

Result: 在14个多模态基准和3个MLLM骨干上进行实验，验证了AutoPrune的有效性、效率和可迁移性。当移除94.4%的视觉token时，AutoPrune保留了超过99%的全token性能，同时将FLOPs降低9.9倍，预填充延迟降低6.4倍。

Conclusion: AutoPrune成功证明了LLM可以自动设计高效且可移植的视觉token剪枝策略，其残差式搜索状态表示是连接LLM内部知识与任务结构约束的关键。这为AI4AI方向提供了新的范式，展示了利用LLM自动优化专用系统设计的可行性。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning can substantially reduce the inference cost of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing methods largely rely on fixed, handcrafted heuristics and costly expert trial and error. As pruning objectives, budgets, and model architectures diversify, manually navigating the expanding design space becomes increasingly difficult. This paper aims to build an AI4AI framework for visual-token pruning by addressing a natural question: Can large language models automatically design effective visual-token reduction algorithms? Although LLMs possess broad algorithmic knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, translating such general knowledge into effective solutions for a specialized task remains nontrivial. We argue that the key lies in designing an appropriate search-state representation that connects the internal knowledge of LLMs with the structural requirements and constraints of visual-token pruning. Based on this insight, we propose AutoPrune, a training-free framework for LLM-driven visual-token pruning policy design. At its core, AutoPrune introduces a Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL) comprising 131 reusable atoms for budget control, token scoring, selection constraints, and token reassembly. A key property of TPDSL is that it represents each search state as a residual modification of a strong base policy. This residual formulation narrows the search space and directs the LLM's attention toward the policy components that are most consequential for performance. Experiments on 14 multimodal benchmarks and three MLLM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability of AutoPrune. Even when removing 94.4% of visual tokens, AutoPrune preserves more than 99% of full-token performance while reducing FLOPs by 9.9x and prefill latency by 6.4x.

</details>


### [195] [Stochastic Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07224)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个二元随机自回归学习的PAC模型，推广了Joshi等人在COLT 2025中提出的确定性自回归学习框架。研究了在三种监督形式（基础一步采样、思维链CoT采样、端到端e2e采样）下学习一步概率和最终token概率所需的最小样本量，发现随机自回归学习与确定性理论在样本复杂度上存在根本差异，并给出了相关上下界及本质紧的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）通过迭代采样next-token分布来生成输出，但现有的自回归学习理论（Joshi等人提出的确定性框架）未能捕捉这种随机采样过程的本质。为了理解在随机自回归生成过程中，不同的监督信号（一步样本、完整轨迹、仅最终token）对学习质量的样本效率有何影响，需要建立一个新的PAC学习理论模型。

Method: 论文提出了一个二元随机自回归学习的PAC框架：一个固定生成器为每个提示字符串分配一个Bernoulli next-token分布，通过M步迭代采样生成输出。定义了三种监督形式：基础一步样本、CoT样本（揭示完整M步随机轨迹）、e2e样本（仅揭示M步轨迹的最终token）。分析了三种监督形式下学习一步概率和最终token概率所需的最小样本量m_base(ε), m_CoT(ε), m_e2e(ε)，并研究了维度为d的逻辑斯蒂函数类在这些模型下的表现。

Result: 主要结果表明：（1）随机自回归学习与确定性理论有根本差异，在相同尺度ε下，m_CoT/m_base和m_e2e/m_CoT可以同时比M/ε（确定性结果的自然类比）任意大，即三种学习任务之间不存在普适的比较关系；（2）但经过尺度调整后，每个类的CoT学习在ε尺度上的样本量可由基础学习在ε/M²尺度上的样本量上界约束；（3）e2e学习在ε尺度上的样本量可由(M/ε)·m_CoT(Θ(ε))界定（至多相差对数因子）；（4）这些依赖关系和尺度本质上是紧的；（5）对d维逻辑斯蒂函数类进行了补充分析。

Conclusion: 随机自回归学习在样本复杂度上呈现出与确定性自回归学习截然不同的行为，不同监督形式之间的差距可以超过确定性理论所预测的范围，但通过适当的尺度调整可以建立对应的上界关系，且这些结果是本质紧的。该工作为从理论层面理解LLM的随机生成过程及其样本效率奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Motivated by LLMs, which generate outputs by iteratively sampling from next-token distributions, we introduce a PAC-learning model for binary stochastic autoregressive learning. This generalizes the deterministic autoregressive learning framework of Joshi et al., COLT 2025. In our model, one fixed generator assigns a Bernoulli next-token distribution to every prompt string. Starting from an input prompt, a token is sampled and appended to the prompt; the same generator is then applied again to this expanded prompt; this procedure is repeated for $M$ steps. Three forms of supervision are considered: base one-step samples, chain-of-thought (CoT) samples that reveal full random trajectories of length $M$, and end-to-end (e2e) samples that reveal only the final token of length $M$ trajectories. For a generator class, we study the minimum number of samples $m_{base}(\varepsilon),m_{CoT}(\varepsilon), m_{e2e}(\varepsilon)$, resp., required to learn the one-step probabilities in the base model, and the final-token probability in the CoT and e2e models, under squared loss error~$\varepsilon$.
  We show that stochastic autoregressive learning fundamentally differs from the deterministic theory. At scale $\varepsilon$, there is no universal comparison between the three learning tasks: both $m_{CoT}/m_{base}$ and $m_{e2e}/m_{CoT}$ can be made simultaneously arbitrarily larger than $M/\varepsilon$, the natural analogue for the existing deterministic results. Nevertheless, after altering scales, for every class, CoT learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded by base learning at scale $\varepsilon/M^2$, whereas e2e learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded, up to logarithmic factors, by $(M/\varepsilon) m_{CoT}(Θ(\varepsilon))$. These dependencies and scales are essentially tight. We complement these bounds by studying dimension $d$ logistic functions in our model.

</details>


### [196] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测线性二次问题中，即使最优策略可表示且价值函数足够表达，标准actor-critic学习仍无法收敛到最优，原因在于critic学习中的偏置而非actor表达能力的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为部分可观测条件下强化学习失败归因于策略表达能力的不足。作者旨在证明即使在可解的情形中，更大的问题在于学习过程本身的偏置，而非策略的表达限制。

Method: 在部分可观测的线性二次问题中，对标准actor-critic学习器进行闭式求解分析。通过数学推导得到策略、代价以及唯一能消除问题的设计选择（学习者的前瞻距离）的闭式表达式，并用深度强化学习实验验证理论预测。

Result: 默认设置下最佳可表示策略仅比理想全观测控制器差10.4%，但学习算法却收敛到比最优可表示策略差35%的策略。critic因无法将观测归因到不可观测的状态部分，误将无法解释的变异解读为自身价值估计的剧烈曲率，actor跟随该误差偏离最优。给agent记忆过去观测并不能帮助，而改变前瞻距离可以解决问题。

Conclusion: 部分可观测强化学习失败的根源在于critic学习的偏置而非actor表达限制；解决方法是调整学习者信任自身价值估计前的预测视界长度，而非增加观测记忆。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [197] [TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07274)
*Yuhan Xie,Jingrong Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TOFD（Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling）框架，利用分割联邦学习（SFL）的架构特性，通过目标推断、样本净化和解耦优化三个阶段，实现对多种投毒攻击的主动检测与鲁棒优化，在五个数据集上优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFL虽然兼具隐私保护和降低客户端计算开销的优势，但其分裂架构引入了独特攻击面，导致易受多种投毒攻击。现有防御方法大多未充分利用分裂范式的特点，难以在早期阶段检测和遏制恶意行为，因此需要一种能够利用分裂架构特性进行主动检测和鲁棒优化的统一防御框架。

Method: TOFD框架包含三个阶段：(1) 目标推断（Target Inference）：通过类别特定的边缘扰动（Margin Perturbation）精炼类级安全区域，识别潜在攻击目标；(2) 样本净化（Sample Purification）：利用跨类最小-最大归一化MP校准阈值，自适应过滤被投毒的smashed data；(3) 解耦优化（Decoupling Optimization）：借助对抗引导模型捕获攻击诱导的模式并在优化中解耦其影响，抑制残余对抗效应。论文还提供了TOFD收敛的理论保证。

Result: 在五个数据集上、多种攻击场景下的广泛实验表明，TOFD一致性地优于现有最先进的防御方法，实现优越的鲁棒性，同时保持较低的计算开销，适合实际部署。

Conclusion: TOFD通过主动利用SFL的分裂架构特性，将目标推断、样本净化和解耦优化统一在一个框架中，实现了对多种投毒攻击的早期检测和鲁棒优化，兼顾了安全性、效率和实用部署可行性。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage.
