EEG's low SNR further suggests many of these tokens are redundant and compressible with little accuracy cost. We propose ZIPBrain, a novel redundancy-aware EEG token pooling module that leverages this low-SNR characteristic to reduce token count. Given a token sequence, ZIPBrain partitions tokens into redundant and unique groups, then merges each redundant token with its most similar counterpart in the unique group. Furthermore, ZIPBrain serves as a training-free, plug-and-play module that seamlessly integrates into standard Transformer encoders with negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments across multiple EEG foundation models show ZIPBrain's strong versatility, achieving 1.3%-10.5% average improvement over baselines, while reducing wall-clock inference time by 32.7% (up to 41.8% with CUDA Graph) compared to the original EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [262] [Not All Problems Are Best Modeled as MILP: A DSL-Centric Framework for Flexible and Accurate Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07040)
*Shaofeng Zhang,Hongyuan Su,Qingwen Peng,Zefang Zong,Shengcai Liu,Ke Tang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiDSL框架，将组合优化问题的建模从僵硬的MILP范式转向领域特定语言（DSL）表示，利用LLM将自然语言映射到标准化的领域结构，解耦问题建模与执行。在44种COP类型基准上，相比MILP管线取得51.66%的公式化准确率提升和91.71%的建模时间减少，并在现有基准上实现23.09%的公式化准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化优化建模框架主要依赖MILP范式，但并非所有问题都适合用MILP建模。将复杂问题域强行转化为线性约束会导致建模复杂度大幅增加，并严重限制求解器的灵活性。因此，需要一种更灵活的建模范式来替代僵化的MILP表示。

Method: OptiDSL框架的核心思想是将问题公式化的重点从MILP转移到领域特定语言（DSL）表示。具体而言，利用LLM将自然语言问题描述映射到标准化、领域接受的DSL结构，从而实现问题建模与执行的解耦，并能无缝集成从传统启发式到现代学习方法的多样化求解器库。

Result: 在包含44种COP类型的综合基准上，OptiDSL相比MILP管线实现了51.66%的公式化准确率提升和91.71%的建模时间减少；在现有基准上还实现了23.09%的公式化准确率提升，全面超越MILP基线。

Conclusion: OptiDSL证明了DSL范式可以有效替代MILP范式用于LLM驱动的组合优化建模，不仅提升了建模准确率，还大幅降低了建模时间成本，同时提升了求解器选择的灵活性，为自动化优化建模提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Solving combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) requires not only efficient algorithms but also carefully crafted formulations. While recent works have leveraged LLMs to automate optimization modeling, current frameworks predominantly rely on a rigid mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) paradigm. In this paper, we argue that not all problems are best modeled as MILP, as forcing complex domains into linear constraints can induce prohibitive modeling complexity and severely restrict solver flexibility. To address this, we propose OptiDSL, a framework that shifts the focus from rigid MILP formulations to domain-specific language (DSL) representations. By utilizing LLMs to map natural language onto standardized, domain-accepted structures, OptiDSL decouples problem formulation from execution. This paradigm enables seamless integration with a diverse library of specialized solvers, ranging from traditional heuristics to modern learning-based methods. Experimental results on the comprehensive benchmark of 44 COP types show that OptiDSL significantly surpasses MILP-based pipelines, yielding a 51.66% gain in formulation accuracy and a 91.71% decrease in modeling time. Notably, it also outperforms MILP-based pipelines on the existing benchmark, achieving a 23.09% higher formulation accuracy. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OptiDSL.

</details>


### [263] [Unsupervised Adaptation of PDE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07053)
*Ziye Song,Zhao Wei,Xin Yu,Ivor Tsang,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督的基于PDE的微调框架，无需真实解即可将预训练的PDE基础模型适配到未见过的方程上。通过物理残差和边界条件构建目标函数，并引入NSLoRA（Newton-Schulz正交化变体）解决标准LoRA在物理量间学习不均衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的PDE基础模型可以跨方程泛化，但适配到未见过的PDE系统通常需要密集的求解数据，而这些数据往往昂贵或不可获得。现有方法对有标注数据的依赖限制了其实际应用。

Method: 1) 在多样化的时变PDE上预训练邻域注意力Transformer，涵盖不同空间尺度以获得可迁移表征；2) 在适配阶段，利用PDE残差和边界条件构建基于物理的目标函数进行无监督微调；3) 通过低秩适配（LoRA）微调模型；4) 提出NSLoRA，一种基于Newton-Schulz正交化的LoRA变体，以解决标准LoRA在物理量之间学习不均衡的问题。

Result: 该方法在无需任何真实解的情况下达到了与有监督LoRA微调相当的性能，并在异构PDE基准（涵盖多个空间维度）上持续优于竞争性的神经算子基线和最新的PDE基础模型。

Conclusion: 无监督的物理驱动微调框架可以有效替代依赖标注数据的监督微调，结合NSLoRA能够实现高效的PDE基础模型适配，在不需要真实解的前提下获得有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Pretrained partial differential equation (PDE) foundation models can generalize across different equations, but adapting them to unseen PDE systems typically requires dense solution data, which is often expensive or unavailable. To address this limitation, we propose an unsupervised PDE-based finetuning framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth solutions. We first pretrain a neighborhood attention Transformer on diverse time-dependent PDEs spanning varying spatial scales, yielding transferable representations across heterogeneous equations. In the adaptation stage, we construct a physics-based objective using the PDE residual and boundary conditions, and finetune the model on unseen equations via low-rank adaptation (LoRA). To address the uneven learning across physical quantities in standard LoRA, we introduce NSLoRA, a Newton-Schulz orthogonalized variant that rebalances adaptation. Our method achieves performance comparable to supervised LoRA finetuning without requiring any ground-truth solutions, while consistently outperforming competitive neural operator baselines and recent PDE foundation models across heterogeneous PDE benchmarks spanning multiple spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [264] [BONSAI: Evolvability-Guided Tree Search over Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07056)
*Yash Priya Shastri,Anand Eswaran,Adnan Qidwai,Pankaj Thorat,Sachin Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BONSAI 是一种新颖的技能优化框架，通过"可进化性"（evolvability）而非单纯的适应度分数来引导文档空间中的搜索。它将技能优化转化为蒙特卡洛搜索树，以变异为边、以上置信界规则选择节点，无需额外代价即可估计变异邻域的可进化性，最终在冻结的 30B 智能体上平均提升 23.13 个百分点的保留准确率，并优于两种预算匹配的基线（GEPA 和 SkillOpt）3.87 和 3.97 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的技能优化方法（保留任何能提升保留分数的编辑）存在盲区：单一分数无法区分"窄的过拟合尖峰"与"宽的稳定平台"，只有后者还能继续改进。因此需要一个能够识别文档空间中可继续进化区域的优化框架。

Method: BONSAI 将技能生长建模为蒙特卡洛搜索树，其中每个子文档是其父文档的一个变异。采用上置信界（UCT）选择规则，其开发项将技能自身适应度与其变异邻域的适应度混合——由于每个子节点都是变异，节点下方的平均分数即可估计该邻域的可进化性，无需额外采样代价；探索项则让当前较弱的分支保持竞争。算法最终输出找到的最佳分数文档，代价与原"接受即改善"（accept-if-better）循环相当。

Result: 在冻结的 30B 参数智能体上，跨三个基准，BONSAI 相比无技能基线将保留准确率平均提升 23.13 个百分点；相比两个预算匹配的基线 GEPA 和 SkillOpt，分别提升 3.87 和 3.97 个百分点。

Conclusion: BONSAI 证明将可进化性（而非即时适应度）作为优化引导信号，是技能优化中一个更有效的原则，能够持续发现可改进的文档区域，同时以与简单局部搜索相当的代价实现更优的最终性能。

Abstract: A skill is a naturallanguage document that steers a frozen agent whose weights cannot be updated so any capability the agent lacks must be supplied in prose Optimising a skill is therefore optimising text against a score and the standard recipe which keeps any edit that raises a heldout score is blind in a specific way a single score cannot tell a document perched on a narrow overfit spike from one resting on a broad plateau even though only the second can still be improved We introduce BONSAI a novel skilloptimisation framework that steers instead by evolvability the capacity of a region of documentspace to keep producing viable variation under further mutation a property biology treats as separate from present fitness BONSAI grows skills as a MonteCarlo search tree in which every child document is a mutation of its parent and descends it under an upperconfidence selection rule whose exploitation term blends a skills own fitness with the fitness of its mutational neighbourhood Because every child is a mutation the mean score recorded beneath a node estimates that neighbourhoods evolvability at no extra cost so the rule concentrates budget on regions that keep improving while its exploration term keeps a currently weak branch in contention BONSAI ships the single bestscoring document it finds at no cost beyond the acceptifbetter loop it replaces With a frozen 30B agent and averaged over three benchmarks BONSAI lifts heldout accuracy over the skillfree agent by 2313 points and improves on two budgetmatched baselines GEPA and SkillOpt by 387 and 397 points respectively

</details>


### [265] [PTQ4SNN: Membrane-Aware Post-Training Quantization for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07066)
*Hui Xie,Tong Shi,Haotong Qin,Aishan Liu,Xiaode Liu,Jinyang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PTQ4SNN是一个基于膜感知的后训练量化框架，联合量化脉冲神经网络的权重和循环膜状态，通过通道级统一尺度桥接和混合精度位分配，在W4量化和约4位膜精度下保持模型精度，无需重训练骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN具备稀疏和事件驱动的计算优势，但其低比特部署不完整：权重量化后，循环膜状态通常仍以浮点保留。量化膜状态具有挑战性，因为其分布跨通道差异大且与前序权重不同，同时接近发射阈值的小扰动可能改变脉冲决策并随时间累积。

Method: 提出PTQ4SNN框架：(1) 通道级统一尺度桥接（Unified Scale Bridge）将膜尺度约束为s_mem,c = s_w,c * 2^k_c，适应膜分布并支持移位兼容的尺度转换；(2) 混合精度位分配（Mixed-Precision Bit Allocation）根据发射活动和量化敏感性，在平均位预算下为膜通道分配2/4/8位精度。框架在可复用的投影-LIF对上进行操作，支持卷积SNN和脉冲驱动Transformer，无需骨干重训练。

Result: 在静态和事件驱动分类以及语义分割任务上的实验表明，PTQ4SNN在W4权重量化和约4位膜精度下有效保持了模型精度。

Conclusion: PTQ4SNN通过膜感知的联合量化策略，成功解决了SNN中膜状态难以量化的挑战，实现了权重与膜状态的完整低比特部署，并且对多种SNN架构通用有效。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) enable sparse and event-driven computation, but their low-bit deployment remains incomplete because recurrent membrane states are commonly retained in floating point even after weight quantization. Quantizing these states is challenging because their distributions differ across channels and from the preceding weights, while small perturbations near the firing threshold may alter spike decisions and accumulate over time. We propose PTQ4SNN, a membrane-aware post-training quantization framework that jointly quantizes weights and recurrent membrane states using only a small calibration set. First, a channel-wise Unified Scale Bridge constrains the membrane scale as s_mem,c = s_w,c * 2^k_c, adapting to membrane distributions while enabling shift-compatible scale conversion. Second, Mixed-Precision Bit Allocation assigns 2/4/8-bit precision to membrane channels according to firing activity and quantization sensitivity under an average-bit budget. The framework operates on reusable projection-LIF pairs and supports both convolutional SNNs and spike-driven Transformers without backbone retraining. Experiments on static and event-based classification and semantic segmentation show that PTQ4SNN effectively preserves model accuracy under W4 quantization and approximately 4-bit membrane precision.

</details>


### [266] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo提出一种基于记忆引导的动态证据探索框架，通过三层次检索状态（文档结构记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情节记忆）结合贝叶斯页面信念更新与Thompson采样，实现跨轮次的动态页面相关性追踪，在3个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档理解需要在数百页中定位稀疏且异质的证据，但现有系统受限于静态检索和脆弱的跨轮记忆。单轮方法依赖固定的top-k页面集合，无法从早期检索错误中恢复；迭代方法虽支持多轮证据获取，但未研究跨轮状态的传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化。

Method: DocMemo维护三层次检索状态：文档架构记忆（捕捉结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关性估计）和问题情节记忆（查询特定的推理轨迹）。推理过程中通过贝叶斯页面信念更新与Thompson采样、空间邻近传播和结构感知的自适应粒度证据访问来持续优化跨轮页面选择，并以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在3个基准数据集上，DocMemo达到最先进的性能，实验验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo通过将长文档推理形式化为动态证据探索，以结构化记忆和贝叶斯信念更新机制克服了静态检索和跨轮记忆脆弱的局限，为长文档理解中的动态证据定位提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [267] [MemOPD: On-Policy Distillation through Memory State Alignment for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07068)
*Zhiyuan Liu,Tinghong Ye,Chenghao Liu,Yizhuo Li,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemOPD（Memory-Aligned On-Policy Distillation），解决长时程智能体在记忆压缩导致上下文重写时，在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中教师评分与学生采样状态不匹配的问题。通过记录每次模型调用的输入输出、恢复原始token位置和因果可见性，并打包重构的调用以高效进行教师评分，MemOPD实现了状态对齐的密集教师监督，同时保留PPO作为最终任务目标。实验表明其F1较PPO提升最高416.2%，打包带来最多1.63倍actor计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在交互过程中上下文不断累积，损害性能与稳定性。紧凑记忆通过压缩和重写历史缓解此问题，但基于PPO的最终任务奖励是稀疏的，对单个记忆更新缺乏指导。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）可提供密集教师监督，然而记忆压缩过程中的上下文重写会破坏"教师在同一状态下评估学生生成动作"的确定性，即动作在来源上属于on-policy，但在状态上可能不再对齐，导致教师评分失效。

Method: MemOPD的核心方法包括：（1）记录每次模型调用的输入和采样输出；（2）恢复各被保留样本原始的token位置和因果可见性，确保重写后的历史不会改变状态语义；（3）将重构的模型调用打包，用于高效的学生-教师批量评分；（4）教师在采样动作位置提供全词表监督，PPO保留最终任务目标，形成双目标联合训练。

Result: 实验验证了多次上下文更新后的状态对齐，MemOPD相对持久历史教师评分在对照实验中F1提升7.0%；MemOPD-3B相对PPO的F1提升最高达416.2%；打包机制带来actor计算最高1.63倍训练加速。

Conclusion: MemOPD通过恢复模型调用的原始状态来维持在线策略蒸馏的合法性，有效解决了记忆压缩引发的状态漂移问题，在任务性能和训练效率两方面都优于基线方法，为长时程智能体的记忆优化提供了新的训练范式和公开实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents accumulate growing contexts during interaction, impairing performance and stability. Compact memory mitigates this problem by compressing and rewriting the history retained between model invocations. Learning what to retain typically relies on proximal policy optimization (PPO) with final task rewards, but sparse rewards provide little guidance for individual memory updates. This limitation motivates on-policy distillation (OPD), which supplies dense teacher supervision on student rollouts. For such supervision to be valid, the teacher must evaluate each sampled action under the same state in which it was generated. However, the context rewriting performed during memory compression can break this alignment. When sampled responses are retained and re-encoded for later invocations, flattening the interaction into a persistent history may cause the teacher to score the action under a state that the student never visited during rollout. The action therefore remains on-policy by provenance, but not necessarily by state. We therefore propose Memory-Aligned On-Policy Distillation (MemOPD). MemOPD records the inputs and sampled outputs of each model invocation, restores its original token positions and causal visibility, and packs the reconstructed invocations for efficient teacher scoring. The teacher provides full-vocabulary supervision at the sampled action positions, while PPO preserves the final task objective. Experiments verify state alignment across several context updates and show that it improves F1 by 7.0% over persistent-history teacher scoring in a matched control. Overall, MemOPD-3B improves F1 over PPO by up to 416.2%, while packing yields up to a 1.63x speedup in actor computation during training. The code for this work is publicly available at: https://github.com/TPssp/MemOPD.

</details>


### [268] [Recipes for Creativity: Iterative Generation and Evaluation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07243)
*Rens Anderson,Tessa Verhoef,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该试点研究将FunSearch适配到食谱生成任务，探索迭代搜索能否提升LLM创造力。结果表明迭代生成-选择可产出与人类基准相当的创造性食谱，但单纯增加迭代次数并不能提升创造力；循环内评估器的设计（特别是选择评分器模型大小）才是影响创造力的首要设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型通常通过单一产物进行评估，而人类创造力往往通过迭代生成、评估和优化的过程产生。作者希望探索迭代搜索是否能够提升LLM的创造力，从而弥合生成式评估与人类创造性过程之间的差距。

Method: 将FunSearch方法适配到2024年Pillsbury Bake-Off食谱生成任务中，使用基于TTCT的LLM评估来衡量输出创造力。设计两个实验，分别测试迭代次数、生成器温度和循环内选择评分器模型大小对创造力得分的影响。

Result: 迭代生成-选择机制能够产生与人类基准创造力得分相当的食谱；但额外增加迭代次数并不提升创造力。循环内评估器的影响最为显著：较小的选择评分器在大多数TTCT维度上产生显著更高的得分；温度除对原创性维度有有限影响外，其余效果不大。

Conclusion: 评估器设计（而非迭代次数或温度）是主观创造性搜索中的一阶设计变量，这为创造性AI系统的设计提供了重要指引——应更多关注评估机制的设计，而非简单增加搜索迭代。

Abstract: Generative models are often evaluated through singular artifacts, whereas human creativity typically emerges through iterative generation, appraisal, and refinement. This pilot study examines whether iterative search improves LLM creativity by adapting FunSearch to recipe generation for the 2024 Pillsbury Bake-Off and evaluating outputs against human benchmarks using TTCT-based LLM evaluation. Across two experiments, we test iteration count, generator temperature, and in-loop selection-scorer model size. Results show that iterative generation-selection can produce recipes with creativity scores comparable to human benchmarks, but additional iterations alone do not improve creativity. The in-loop evaluator matters most: a smaller selection scorer yields significantly higher scores across most TTCT dimensions, while temperature has limited effects except for originality. These findings suggest that evaluator design is a first-order design variable in subjective creative search.

</details>


### [269] [Transformers Struggle to Use Their Emergent World Models: Revisiting the Tower of Hanoi, and the Illusion of Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07077)
*Devin Pereira,Willem Zuidema*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了小规模Transformer和大型推理模型（LRMs）在汉诺塔（塔变体）任务上的表现。小Transformer在训练中涌现出几何上忠实的世界模型（Sierpinski三角）表征；而两个前沿推理模型（Qwen3.6-27B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B）虽然在提示词末尾能近乎完美地编码该世界模型，但在超过3个环时仍然失败。研究发现失败根源在于规划过程中世界模型表征的衰减，而非缺失——通过注入提示时刻的表征可以部分恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂推理任务（尤其是需要规划的任务）上表现不佳已广为人知，但此前缺乏对其失败机制的深入理解。作者希望从机制层面解释：大型推理模型（LRMs）在汉诺塔这类规划难题上为什么失败——是因为从未建立正确的世界模型，还是建立了却在推理过程中丢失了？这一问题的答案将直接决定改进大模型推理能力的策略方向。

Method: （1）从零训练小规模Transformer，在预计算的解轨迹上学习汉诺塔任务；（2）利用多种可解释性技术（线性探针等）分析小Transformer内部表征，验证其是否形成可线性解码的、几何上忠实于状态空间（Sierpinski三角）的世界模型，并通过干预实验验证因果性；（3）将同样的探针技术应用于两个大型推理模型（Qwen3.6-27B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B），在其扩展思维链过程中不同阶段探测世界模型表征的质量；（4）通过推理时注入提示时刻的表征进行因果性验证，观察性能是否改善。

Result: （1）小Transformer成功发展出可线性解码、几何忠实于Sierpinski三角形状的世界模型表征，且该表征因果性地参与了解题过程；（2）两个大型推理模型在提示词末尾（推理开始时）能近乎完美地编码Sierpinski世界模型，但在超过3个环的任务上仍然大比例失败；（3）失败根源定位为规划过程中世界模型表征的逐渐衰减（而非缺失）；（4）在推理过程中注入提示时刻的表征可显著改善性能，证实了"表征维持失败"假设。

Conclusion: 大型推理模型在汉诺塔任务上的失败并非因为无法构建世界模型——它们确实构建了，而是在长链式推理过程中无法维持该表征。这一发现将先前报道的性能崩溃重新框定为"可持续性"问题而非"能力"问题，为改进大型推理模型提供了新思路：重点应放在推理过程中表征的稳定与维持，而非增强初始理解能力。

Abstract: The Tower of Hanoi is a simple planning puzzle that in prior work has proven challenging for large reasoning models (LRMs). Current models solve the standard formulation of the puzzle, but still struggle with the flat-to-flat variant (where initial and goal states are not restricted to have all rings on a single peg). This paper presents an in-depth study of how both small, in-house Transformers and large, third-party LRMs solve this task. To understand the failures mechanistically, we first train small Transformers from scratch on precomputed solution traces. Using a variety of interpretability techniques, we show that these Transformers develop an emergent world model: a linearly decodable, geometrically faithful representation of the puzzle's state space (the Sierpinski triangle), that is causally involved in solving the puzzles. Second, we return to the large LLMs and apply our techniques to two frontier reasoning models, Qwen3.6-27B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-32B, that attempt to solve the task through extended chain-of-thought. Surprisingly, we find that both models encode the Sierpinski world model near-perfectly at the end of the prompt, and yet fail at the majority of tasks when there are more than 3 rings. We locate the source of this failure in the decaying representation of the world model. We probe for the representation at different stages during planning, and establish causality by showing that performance can be improved by injecting the prompt-time representation at inference. The failure of the models is thus one of maintenance of the required representations, not their absence, and performance is at least partially recoverable. These results thus reframe the reported collapse in performance from prior work: current Large Reasoning Models build a world model, and then lose it.

</details>


### [270] [MemWM: Memory-Augmented Text-Based World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07107)
*Yujun Wang,Tao Zhang,Jinhe Bi,Aniri,Wenxuan Ye,Boliang Liu,Sikuan Yan,Shuning Wang,Xuebing Zhou,Sören Pirk,Hinrich Schütze,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemWM是一种记忆增强的基于文本的世界模型，通过引入世界记忆（包括转移规则、状态缓存和难以预测的事实）来条件化下一状态想象，从而解决世界模型在规划任务中的系统性预测错误。它引入了结构化状态保真度（SSF）指标来评估事实状态保持，并在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上验证了记忆增强智能体在规划上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在智能体规划中日益重要，但现有的流畅下一状态预测仍可能遗漏任务关键事实、破坏产品属性或应用错误的转移规则，导致系统性预测错误。这些错误会直接影响下游规划质量，因此需要一种方法来解决此类预测偏差。

Method: MemWM包含两大核心组件：（1）世界记忆——一个精选的记忆库，包含转移规则、状态缓存和难以预测的事实，用于条件化下一状态的想象；（2）策略侧世界技能——在完整规划设置中，通过检索任务级技能和逐步纠错的指导信息来辅助动作选择，同时保持策略模型冻结。评估方面引入结构化状态保真度（SSF）指标，通过基准特定的事实和字段对预测状态进行评分。

Result: 与SFT基线相比，记忆增强训练在SSF上最高提升206.3%；在完整规划设置中，记忆增强智能体相比SFT训练的世界模型智能体，在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上的下游成功率最高提升65.4%。敏感性分析进一步表明，检索记忆在不同记忆和动作预算设置下都能提升任务成功率与效率。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的世界模型能够有效缓解系统性预测错误，提升事实状态保真度，并通过提供策略侧技能与纠错指导来增强规划性能。该方法在多种环境和不同资源预算下均展现出稳健的改进效果。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used to support planning in agents by predicting how environment states evolve in response to agent actions. Yet fluent next-state predictions can still omit task-critical facts, corrupt product attributes, or apply incorrect transition rules. To address such systematic prediction errors, we introduce MemWM, a memory-augmented text-based world model. MemWM uses world memory, a curated memory bank of transition rules, state caches, and hard-to-predict facts, to condition next-state imagination. We evaluate factual state preservation with Structured State Fidelity (SSF), which scores predicted states through benchmark-specific facts and fields. Compared with SFT, memory-augmented training improves SSF by up to 206.3%. In the full planning setting, we keep the policy model frozen and provide policy-side world skill: retrieved task-level skills and step-wise corrective guidance for action selection. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, memory-augmented agents improve downstream success over an SFT-trained world-model agent, with up to a 65.4% relative gain. Sensitivity analyses further show that retrieved memory improves task success and efficiency under different memory and action-budget settings.

</details>


### [271] [GeoBenchLLM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs on Geo-Related Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07411)
*Rodrigo Ferreira Rodrigues,Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoBenchLLM，一个用于探测大语言模型在地理相关任务上泛化能力的综合基准，基于12个公开数据集评估多个LLM的地理空间和时间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地理数据大语言模型研究大多在单一同质化环境中进行，限制了对其泛化能力的深入理解，缺乏一个全面的基准来系统评估LLM在地理相关任务上的表现。

Method: 精心挑选了12个来自不同地理任务和领域的公开数据集，构建了一个综合基准，并在该基准上评估了多个大语言模型在地理空间和时间理解方面的能力。

Result: 研究结果表明，推理能力和模型规模对整体性能有显著影响。

Conclusion: GeoBenchLLM作为一个公开可用的基准（https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM），为评估大语言模型在地理空间和时间理解方面的能力提供了标准化框架，并揭示了推理与规模对性能的关键作用。

Abstract: In the context of geodata, existing Large Language Models have often been studied in a homogeneous setting, which has considerably limited insights into their generalization capabilities. In this paper, we present \benchName, a comprehensive benchmark for probing LLMs on geo-related tasks. We leverage a careful selection of twelve publicly available datasets from diverse geo-related tasks and domains, and evaluate a set of LLMs on geo-spatial and temporal understanding using our benchmark. Our results show that reasoning and size have a strong impact on overall performance. GeoBenchLLM is publicly available at https://github.com/Rfr2003/GeoBenchLLM.

</details>


### [272] [How Much, Then Where: Credit-Conserving Action-to-Token Allocation for Multi-Turn Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07118)
*Lichao Ma,Yang Sun,Shuaitao Zhao,Yangyi Fang,Cong Qin,Xiaoliang Fu,Yuhang Tian,Yuchen Wei,Junbo Zhu,Yang Wei,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FACTOR，一种将多轮智能体强化学习中的信用分配解耦为两层（轨迹级动作信用与动作级token信用）的方法。通过检查点校准的TD残差分配动作信用，并通过反馈条件下的师生似然差距分配token信用，在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上均一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体强化学习中信用分配存在两个层级：轨迹层面对动作的信用分配，以及单个动作内部跨token的信用分配。现有方法未明确解耦这两层决策，导致信用分配不准确，且动作的标量替代权重隐式依赖于token长度。

Method: FACTOR包含三个核心组件：(1) 使用检查点校准的TD残差为每个动作分配信用，使其望远镜式求和等于轨迹优势；(2) 使用反馈条件下的师生似然差距将每个动作的信用分配至其实际生成的token；(3) 采用per-action归一化保持动作平均系数并防止token级符号翻转，同时配合action-mean reduction消除动作标量替代权重对token长度的隐式依赖。在行为策略且裁剪前，每个动作的内部action-mean替代项等于其TD信用。

Result: FACTOR在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个环境中一致优于竞争基线，所有环境-种子组合的对比均偏向FACTOR，且在最长horizon环境中提升最大。相同的超参数无需重新调优即可迁移到更大的骨干网络和不同的模型家族。消融实验表明TD动作信用是主要改进驱动因素，事后token分配提供互补增益。

Conclusion: FACTOR通过解耦轨迹级动作信用分配和动作级token信用分配，提供了一种更精确且可迁移的多轮智能体强化学习信用分配框架，其一致性和跨环境、跨模型的稳定性验证了该设计的有效性。

Abstract: Credit assignment in multi-turn agent reinforcement learning operates at two levels: assigning trajectory-level credit to actions and distributing each action's credit across its tokens. In this paper, we introduce FACTOR, which separates these decisions. FACTOR uses checkpoint-calibrated TD residuals to assign per-action credits that telescope to the trajectory advantage, and feedback-conditioned teacher-student likelihood gaps to allocate each credit across the realized action tokens. Per-action normalization preserves the action-average coefficient and prevents token-level sign flips. We pair this construction with an action-mean reduction, removing the implicit dependence of an action's scalar surrogate weight on its token length. At the behavior policy and before clipping, each action's inner action-mean surrogate equals its TD credit. FACTOR consistently improves over competitive baselines across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, with every environment-seed comparison favoring FACTOR and the largest gains emerging on the longest-horizon environment. The same hyperparameters transfer without retuning to a larger backbone and to a different model family. Ablations identify TD action credit as the dominant driver of the improvement, with hindsight token allocation contributing complementary gains.

</details>


### [273] [ResidencyRL: Reinforcement Learning in Simulated Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07418)
*Valentin Liévin,Samuel Schmidgall,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Akshay Goel,Anil Palepu,Chunjong Park,Vahid Balazadeh,Min Woo Sun,Marius Guerard,Justin Chen,Dave Steiner,Vikram Dhillon,Ibrahim Azar,Akhil Mehta,Nicholas Spetsieris,Shilpan Shah,Maen Abdelrahim,Amit Dahiya,Yun Liu,Katherine Chou,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale R. Webster,Quoc V. Le,Raia Hadsell,Joelle Barral,Carey Radebaugh,Aleksandra Faust,Shekoofeh Azizi,Mike Schaekermann,Po-Hsuan Cameron Chen,Tao Tu,David Racz,Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResidencyRL是一种通过模拟多轮临床对话（最多60轮对话和8次工具调用）进行强化学习训练临床AI代理的方法。该代理在对抗性条件下诊断准确率提升7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），漏诊红旗信号率降低31%，盲法临床专家在87.6%的对比中更偏好该训练代理，且能力可迁移至未见过的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在静态医学基准上表现优异，但缺乏针对完整临床决策序列的优化方法。临床推理本质上依赖于多轮医患对话——采集病史、完善诊断假设、在不确定下制定管理方案，因此需要开发能够优化整个临床决策序列的训练方法。

Method: 提出ResidencyRL框架：将策略代理与具备复杂对抗行为能力的大语言模型模拟器配对，通过强化学习在模拟的多轮临床交互（每轨迹最多60轮对话和8次工具调用）中训练代理。训练使用结构化奖励信号，涵盖诊断准确性、管理质量、沟通、文档记录和安全性五个维度。

Result: 在保留评估中，ResidencyRL代理在对抗条件下诊断准确率提升7.0%（88.0% vs 81.0%），漏诊红旗信号率降低31%（有效缓解过早闭合问题）。盲法临床专家在87.6%的并排对比中偏好训练后的代理。能力泛化至未见过基准：在AMIE多访视基准的全部六个临床维度上超越基础模型，在AgentClinic和CRAFT-MD上呈现一致的方向性改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，顺序临床决策能力可以通过多轮强化学习在模拟环境中有效习得，产生稳健且可泛化的能力，为临床掌握铺平道路。但需通过真实临床工作流的前瞻性验证来确认其临床实用性。

Abstract: In medical education, physicians convert academic knowledge into clinical expertise through residency: years of training across thousands of encounters, with diverse sources of feedback and progressively greater autonomy. Much of clinical reasoning relies on the patient encounter, a dialogue in which a clinician elicits history, refines diagnostic hypotheses, and decides management under uncertainty. While large language models (LLMs) excel on static medical benchmarks, methods to optimize the full sequence of clinical decisions remain underdeveloped. We present ResidencyRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) method for training clinical artificial intelligence (AI) agents through simulated multi-turn clinical encounters (up to 60 dialogue turns and 8 tool calls per trajectory). ResidencyRL pairs the policy agent with LLM simulators capable of complex, adversarial behaviors, training against a structured reward aligned to diagnostic accuracy, management quality, communication, documentation, and safety. On held-out evaluations, the ResidencyRL agent improves diagnostic accuracy by 7.0% under adversarial conditions (88.0% vs. 81.0%) and reduces missed red flag rates by 31%, demonstrating rigorous mitigation of premature closure. Blinded expert clinicians validated these gains, preferring the trained agent in 87.6% of side-by-side comparisons. The procedural competencies transfer to unseen benchmarks: the agent outperforms the base model across all six clinical axes of the AMIE multi-visit benchmark, and shows consistent directional improvements on AgentClinic and CRAFT-MD. Our findings demonstrate that sequential clinical decision-making can be effectively learned through multi-turn RL in simulation, yielding robust, generalizable capabilities, paving the way towards clinical mastery. Prospective validation with real-world workflows remains necessary to establish clinical utility.

</details>


### [274] [DiDPO: Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization for Coding Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07147)
*Xucong Wang,Zhe Zhao,Liheng Yu,Di Wu,Xiaofeng Cao,Pengkun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization (DiDPO)，一种无评论家的强化学习方法，通过从代码diff的结构中构建细粒度信用单元来解决编码智能体中多步骤、多区域同时修改导致的信用分配难题。DiDPO将多轮交互组织为思考-行动步骤，通过"可分组性分数"拆分diff并聚类相似子diff形成优势组，将diff级优势映射回token级。实验表明在Qwen2.5-7B-Coder上显著优于强基线，并开源了verl-code代码库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR方法大多依赖结果奖励或步骤级奖励，无法深入代码diff内部，使得编码任务中同一代码版本内不同区域同时修改的独立贡献不可区分，信用分配粒度不细，编码动作的独特性质对训练不可见。

Method: DiDPO采用无评论家（critic-free）设计：首先将多轮编码交互组织为多个思考-行动步骤；然后从采样轨迹中发现代码diff，通过"groupability score"将每个整体diff拆分为高度相似的子diff并选择锚点（aggregating anchors），该分数用于优化拆分模式以平衡锚点的语义范围与形成组的质量；最终形成优势组并将diff级优势投影回单个响应token上。

Result: 在长时程编码和推理基准上，DiDPO显著优于强agentic RL基线。在Qwen2.5-7B-Coder上，DiDPO比可比方法高出10%以上，缩小了与更大模型的差距。此外开源了verl-code，一个支持多种RL方法和编码基准的agentic RL代码库。

Conclusion: DiDPO为编码智能体训练提供了原则性的细粒度信用分配框架，证明了从代码diff结构出发进行信用构建的有效性，为编码agent训练中的RLVR方法提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for training coding agents, where the execution feedback from compilation and tests provides objective verification. However, unlike agent tasks, coding agents face a unique and finer-grained credit assignment challenge: at each step, coding actions simultaneously pack varying changes into different regions of a code version, which makes the contribution of independent change indistinguishable. Existing RLVR methods mostly leverage the outcome reward or step-level reward, which fails to dive into a code diff and makes unique properties of coding actions invisible to training. In this paper, we propose Diff-in-Diff Policy Optimization (DiDPO), a critic-free RL method that constructs fine-grained credit units directly from the structure of code diffs. DiDPO organizes multi-turn coding interactions into multiple thought--action steps and discovers code diffs across sampled trajectories. It then selects anchors by aggregating highly similar sub-diffs split from each whole diff by our ``groupability score'', which provides the splitting schema that optimally balances the semantic scope of anchors and the group mass they may form. Finally these anchors form advantage groups and project the diff-level advantage back to individual response tokens. Experiments on long-horizon coding and reasoning benchmarks show that DiDPO significantly outperforms strong agentic RL baselines. On Qwen2.5-7B-Coder, DiDPO exceeds comparable methods by over 10\% and narrows the gap with far larger models, offering a principled framework for fine-grained credit assignment in coding agent training. We also open-source verl-code, an agentic rl codebase that supports various RL methods and coding benchmarks.

</details>


### [275] [A MARL Centered Reference Architecture for Large Language Model Augmentation in Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07148)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了大语言模型（LLM）在协同多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架下用于现代制造业自适应控制的四类接入点（策略、奖励设计、智能体间通信、分层规划），提出基于证据的三层MARL中心参考架构，并给出了LLM增强的Dec-POMDP描述性比较记法。结论认为：常规MARL更适合高频、结构化、分布式的具体任务协调，而LLM更适合语义解释、奖励起草、人机交互和慢速监督规划；纯LLM控制器目前尚不能等效满足严格的实时、分布式、安全关键控制需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造业对自适应控制提出了六重耦合需求：局部决策需考虑全局后果、部分可观测性、非平稳性、需兼顾反射级快速响应与长期影响、延迟且分散的回报、以及难以显式建模的动力学。这些需求恰好与中心化训练-去中心化执行的Dec-POMDP形式的协同MARL自然匹配。然而，LLM在其中的定位不清晰，现有文献缺乏对LLM应增强、接口、训练或替换MARL协调核心的系统性分析框架。

Method: 本文采用MARL中心的视角，提出一个四接点分类法（策略、奖励设计、智能体间通信、分层规划）来组织文献；建立基于条件的能力画像（原生机制、报告性能、形式化保证、工程成熟度）；进行部署就绪度分析以识别各角色背后的证据；最终提出三层MARL中心参考架构，并引入LLM增强的Dec-POMDP作为描述性比较记法，记录四种接入选择而不引入新的决策过程类别或算法。

Result: 主要贡献是提出了一个基于证据的三层MARL中心参考架构，分别对应语义推理层、自适应协同控制层和独立保障执行层。文献证据表明：常规MARL在任务特定训练后更适合高频、结构化、去中心化的协调；LLM组件在语义解释、奖励起草、人机交互和较慢速的监督规划方面有前景。而纯LLM制造控制器尚不能证明其对严格实时、去中心化、安全关键控制的等效性。

Conclusion: LLM在制造业自适应控制中的角色应定位为MARL核心的增强组件而非替代者，具体体现在语义层和监督层；该结论受现有证据范围的约束，不断言LLM控制器的不可行性，但当前纯LLM方案在实时安全关键控制上的能力差距是明确的。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing imposes six coupled demands on adaptive control: local decisions with global consequences, partial observability, nonstationarity, reflex speed response with long horizon effects, delayed and diffuse outcomes, and dynamics that resist explicit modeling. Cooperative multiagent reinforcement learning (MARL), posed as a Dec-POMDP under centralized training with decentralized execution, is a particularly natural formalism for these demands. This paper adopts a MARL centered scope and asks where large language models (LLMs) should augment, interface with, train, or, in the strongest competitive case, replace that coordination core. A taxonomy organizes the literature through four LLM attachment points: policy, reward design, communication between agents, and hierarchical planning. A conditional capability profile separates native mechanism, reported performance, formal guarantee, and engineering maturity, and a deployment readiness analysis identifies the evidence behind each role. These stages yield the principal contribution: a three layer MARL centered reference architecture, grounded in evidence, for semantic reasoning, adaptive cooperative control, and independently assured execution. The LLM-Augmented Dec-POMDP is a descriptive comparative notation for that architecture, recording four attachment choices without introducing a new decision process class or algorithm. Under the reviewed evidence, conventional MARL is better suited to frequent, structured, decentralized coordination after task specific training, whereas LLM components are promising for semantic interpretation, reward drafting, human interaction, and slower supervisory planning. Current LLM only manufacturing controllers do not yet establish equivalence for strict real time, decentralized, safety critical control; this conclusion is bounded by the available evidence and does not assert impossibility.

</details>


### [276] [PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438)
*Mohammad Amanlou,Parham Abed Azad,Farbod Davoodi,Mostafa Masumi,Behnam Bahrak,Abdol-Hossein Vahabie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PsychoAgent，一种面向LLM智能体的认知架构，将事实性记忆与情感性记忆分离，并通过冲突感知的执行控制器进行整合。在三个受控冲突场景中，该架构检索到更多冲突关键记忆（0.933 vs. 基线的0.500和0.667），尽管存在轻微语义相似度代价。五名盲评者评估显示完整架构均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的成对差异不显著。三天追踪演示了持久情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆加权，支持情感敏感检索作为建模类人冲突效应的可检查机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人认知并非仅依据主题相似性选择过往经验，情感显著性和未解决的冲突同样影响信息的可及性。现有LLM智能体的记忆机制缺乏对情感维度与冲突效应的建模，作者旨在填补这一空白，构建更接近人类认知架构的智能体记忆系统。

Method: 提出PsychoAgent认知架构：(1) 将记忆分为事实性记忆与情感性记忆两个分离的存储系统；(2) 情感记忆先经过语义相关性过滤，再按显著性（salience）重新排序，保留主题契合度的同时让情感重要的痕迹进入提示；(3) 通过冲突感知的执行控制器（conflict-aware executive controller）整合两类记忆。

Result: 在三个受控冲突场景中，完整架构的冲突关键记忆检索准确率达0.933，优于语义-情感基线（0.500）和单记忆RAG基线（0.667），但承受了少量语义相似度损失。五名盲评者对27个输出的评估显示，经评分者内标准化后完整架构整体均值最高（+0.22 SD），但校正后的成对比较差异不显著。三天追踪演示了持久情感、离线记忆重组和选择性记忆加权现象。

Conclusion: 情感敏感检索可作为一种可检查（inspectable）的机制，用于在LLM智能体中建模类人冲突效应。该架构通过分离事实与情感记忆并引入冲突感知控制，使智能体能够在保持语义相关性的同时优先检索情感上重要的记忆痕迹。

Abstract: Human-like cognition does not select past experience by topical similarity alone: affective significance and unresolved conflict also shape what becomes accessible. We present PsychoAgent, a cognitive architecture for LLM agents that separates factual and affective memory and integrates both through a conflict-aware executive controller. Affective memories are first filtered by semantic relevance and then re-ranked by salience, preserving topical fit while allowing emotionally important traces to enter the prompt. Across three controlled conflict scenarios, the full architecture retrieved more conflict-critical memories than semantic-affective and single-memory RAG baselines (0.933 vs. 0.500 and 0.667), with a small semantic-similarity cost. Five blinded raters evaluated 27 outputs. After within-rater standardization, the full architecture had the highest overall mean (+0.22 SD), but corrected pairwise differences were not significant. A three-day illustrative trace further shows persistent affect, offline memory recombination, and selective memory reweighting. The findings support affect-sensitive retrieval as an inspectable mechanism for modeling human-like conflict effects in LLM agents.

</details>


### [277] [NiyamAI - An Intent-Bound AI Agent with Cryptographically Verifiable Guardrails using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07167)
*Aditya Katkar,Om Karkele,Kartik Mandhane,Manisha More,Yash Kashid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Niyam-AI是一个通过zk-SNARK零知识证明使LLM智能体安全强制可验证的框架，在Agent-SafetyBench上F1达88.5%，误报率仅1.1%，远超现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体面临提示注入、幻觉推理和不安全工具调用三大攻击面，现有防御依赖在同一台机器上运行的系统提示或策略过滤器，无法提供可验证的执行证明。需要一种能够数学上可验证的安全强制机制。

Method: 会话开始时将允许的工具和约束锁定为通过SHA-256提交的意图合同（Intent Contract）；每个工具调用由隔离的Judge模型拦截和验证；通过后通过EZKL生成zk-SNARK证明；只有证明验证通过后工具才执行，使第三方无需访问Judge模型权重即可确认强制执行。

Result: 在2,000个Agent-SafetyBench真实场景中，与NeMo Guardrails、Llama Prompt Guard 2和GPT-OSS-Safeguard使用5折分层交叉验证比较，F1达88.5%，误报率1.1%（bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%]）。McNemar配对检验显示显著优势（p<0.0001）。证明生成耗时2260.6±218.4ms/次，验证耗时53.1±11.8ms。

Conclusion: Niyam-AI提供了既高度准确又数学可验证的安全护栏，但需注意其评估中分类器是针对Agent-SecurityBench适配的，且基线为零样本，这一差异在Section IV.C中讨论。

Abstract: Giving an AI agent the ability to send emails, query databases, or execute commands is useful--until the agent is tricked into doing something it shouldn't. Prompt injection, hallucinated reasoning, and unsafe tool calls form the primary attack surface for autonomous LLM agents. Existing defenses rely on software checks like system prompts or policy filters running on the same machine the attacker targets, offering no verifiable proof of execution. We introduce Niyam-AI, a framework that makes safety enforcement provable. At session start, permitted tools and constraints are locked into an Intent Contract committed via SHA-256. Every tool call is intercepted and validated by an isolated Judge model; upon passing, a zk-SNARK proof is generated via EZKL.
  The tool executes only after proof verification, allowing third parties to confirm enforcement without accessing Judge model weights. Evaluating Niyam-AI on 2,000 real-world scenarios from Agent-SafetyBench against NeMo Guardrails, Meta's Llama Prompt Guard 2, and OpenAI's GPT-OSS-Safeguard using 5-fold stratified cross-validation yields an F1 score of 88.5% with a 1.1% false-positive rate (bootstrap 95% CI: [85.19%, 91.88%], N=1000). McNemar's exact paired test confirms significant improvement: Niyam-AI wins 390 discordant scenarios against NeMo (vs 20 losses), 115 against Prompt Guard 2 (vs 13), and 384 against GPT-OSS-Safeguard (vs 19) with p < 0.0001 in all cases.
  Proof generation adds 2260.6 +/- 218.4 ms per approved action, while verification takes 53.1 +/- 11.8 ms. Niyam-AI provides a guardrail that is both highly accurate and mathematically verifiable--though this reflects a classifier adapted to Agent-SafetyBench evaluated against zero-shot baselines, a distinction discussed in Section IV.C.

</details>


### [278] [SkillProx: Self-Evolving Agent Skills via Proximal Textual Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07449)
*Mingxuan Zheng,Yujin Zhou,Chuxue Cao,Boqin Yin,Yuyao Zhang,Jiapeng Sun,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillProx是一个基于近端梯度思想的技能学习框架，通过闭环诊断演化与实用感知的近端精炼相结合，在多个LLM骨干网络上平均准确率提升3.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体技能学习方法缺乏显式的诊断-结果反馈机制，且将删除操作视为通用编辑而非专门的知识整合机制，导致技能积累与精炼效率不足。

Method: SkillProx采用近端梯度启发的forward-backward框架：前向阶段在相同任务批次上重新执行诊断驱动的编辑，回滚性能退化并反馈测量结果；后向阶段将技能分解为可审计的知识单元，通过留一法效用审计估计贡献，并应用验证门控的整合、降级或移除操作。

Result: 在分布内与分布外基准上，SkillProx较最强梯度基线平均准确率提升3.0个百分点；组件消融实验验证了闭环诊断与近端精炼的互补效果。

Conclusion: SkillProx通过将删除机制专门化并引入闭环诊断反馈，有效提升了LLM智能体的技能积累与精炼能力，表明近端梯度思想可有效迁移到文本空间的技能学习场景。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly adapt to recurring tasks by accumulating procedural knowledge in skills. These skills are lightweight, reusable textual artifacts that are loaded into the agent's context without weight updates. Recent methods refine skills through iterative task execution, failure diagnosis, and trajectory-guided text-space updates. However, existing frameworks lack explicit diagnosis--outcome feedback and treat deletion as a generic edit operation rather than a dedicated mechanism for consolidating accumulated knowledge. We introduce SkillProx, a proximal-gradient-inspired forward--backward framework that couples closed-loop diagnostic evolution with utility-aware proximal refinement. Motivated by a composite objective balancing task loss and skill complexity, the forward stage re-executes diagnosis-driven edits on the same task batch, rolls back regressions, and feeds measured outcomes into subsequent diagnoses. The backward stage decomposes the resulting skill into auditable knowledge units, estimates their contributions using a frozen leave-one-out utility audit, and applies validation-gated consolidation, demotion, or removal. Experiments on in-distribution and out-of-distribution benchmarks across multiple backbone LLMs show that SkillProx improves average accuracy by 3.0 percentage points over the strongest gradient-based baseline. Component ablations demonstrate the complementary effects of closed-loop diagnosis and proximal refinement.

</details>


### [279] [Agent Memory Distillation: Empowering Small LLM Agents with Hierarchical Teacher Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07169)
*Taeil Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Memory Distillation (AMD)，一种无需训练的框架，通过从大模型教师智能体向小模型学生智能体传递结构化知识（工作流记忆、子任务记忆、函数记忆三种层级记忆），在三个工具使用基准上平均提升小模型准确率最高达27.2个百分点，显著优于现有基于记忆的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆系统已被证明能提升智能体性能，但对于小语言模型而言潜力尚未被充分探索，因为小模型难以自行生成足够数量的成功轨迹。作者希望解决这一问题，将大模型教师的知识高效迁移给小模型学生。

Method: AMD是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，包含三种互补的记忆类型：(1) 工作流记忆（Workflow memory）编码任务级策略；(2) 子任务记忆（Subtask memory）在中粒度提供具体行为示例；(3) 函数记忆（Function memory）捕获每个函数调用的约定和常见陷阱。工作流和子任务记忆在任务开始时主动注入，函数记忆则在工具调用出错时被动检索。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准（AppWorld、BFCL V3、ToolSandbox）上，使用四个4B-8B参数的学生模型，以GPT-5-mini为教师，平均准确率分别提升27.2%、11.2%和3.4%。在所有基于记忆的基线方法中表现一致更好。进一步分析发现：子任务记忆贡献最大；教师有效性取决于教师能力和学生兼容性；4B规模的学生受益最多。

Conclusion: AMD通过分层记忆蒸馏有效提升了小语言模型在工具使用任务上的表现，无需训练即可实现知识迁移。子任务记忆是关键组件，且AMD对较小规模的学生模型尤为有效。

Abstract: Memory systems have shown promise for improving agent performance, but their potential remains largely unexplored for small language models, which struggle to generate sufficient successful trajectories on their own. We propose Agent Memory Distillation (AMD), a training-free framework that transfers structured knowledge from a large teacher agent to a small student agent through hierarchical memory. AMD constructs three complementary memory types from successful teacher trajectories: Workflow memory encodes task-level strategies, Subtask memory provides concrete behavioral examples at an intermediate granularity, and Function memory captures per-function calling conventions and common pitfalls. Workflow and Subtask memories are injected proactively at the start of each task, while Function memory is retrieved reactively upon tool-calling errors. We evaluate AMD on three tool-use benchmarks using four student models (4B-8B parameters) with GPT-5-mini as the teacher, achieving average accuracy gains of 27.2%p, 11.2%p, and 3.4%p on AppWorld, BFCL V3, and ToolSandbox, while consistently outperforming existing memory-based baselines. Further analysis shows that Subtask memory contributes the largest gains, teacher effectiveness depends on both teacher capability and student compatibility, and 4B-sized students benefit most from AMD.

</details>


### [280] [SetEasy: A Multi-Modal Classroom Engagement Assessment and Seating Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07188)
*Zhihao Xie,Hongye Yang,Shien Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetEasy系统通过在固定座位网格中融合多模态感知（腕带生理数据、4K视频、环境数据）和基于修订版ISEQ的v-Gage模型，每周生成两周课堂参与度预测并映射到学生-座位效用矩阵，再利用CP-SAT在视觉可达性和社会动态约束下生成最优排座方案。在23名学生、331节课的四周部署中，v-Gage在情感、行为、认知及整体维度上收敛，RMSE从0.75降至0.53；优化后平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，超过三分之二的座位达到高参与度，后排低活跃模式显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统固定座位网格的课堂环境中，学生参与度往往因座位布局不理想而受限，尤其是后排座位的学生参与度普遍较低。现有研究缺乏一种无需更换硬件、可解释且可迁移的座位优化方案。作者旨在通过数据驱动的多模态感知与优化结合，为固定座位课堂提供可持续的参与度提升路径，并应对全球课堂情境同质化背景下对文化响应性空间设计的迫切需求。

Method: SetEasy采用"多模态感知+优化"的双阶段框架。第一阶段融合腕带生理信号、4K视频以及环境数据，训练基于修订版ISEQ（学参与度模型）的v-Gage模型。第二阶段每周将两周的参与度预测映射为学生-座位效用矩阵，使用CP-SAT求解器在视觉可达性约束（保证学生视线通畅）和社会动力学约束（如同伴关系、互动模式）下生成排座方案。整个系统在真实课堂中进行持续迭代优化。

Result: 在23名学生、331节课的四周部署实验中，v-Gage模型在情感、行为、认知及整体四个维度上均收敛，预测RMSE从0.75大幅降至0.53。经过CP-SAT优化生成的排座方案，课堂平均参与度从0.30跃升至0.70；超过三分之二的座位达到了高参与度水平，同时后排座位的低活跃参与模式显著减少。

Conclusion: 研究证实，在不改变硬件条件的前提下，基于多模态感知的可解释数据驱动排座策略能够显著提升课堂参与度。该"评估+优化"范式具有可迁移性和可持续性，为文化响应式、差异化空间设计提供了全新路径，对应对全球课堂空间设计同质化趋势具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: SetEasy optimizes classroom engagement in fixed seating grids. It fuses multimodal sensing (wristband physiology, 4K video, environmental data) and trains a v-Gage model grounded in a revised ISEQ. Each week, two-week engagement forecasts are mapped to a student-seat utility matrix, and CP-SAT generates seating plans under visual-access and social-dynamics constraints. In a four-week deployment (23 students, 331 classes), v-Gage converged across affective, behavioral, cognitive, and overall dimensions, cutting RMSE from 0.75 to 0.53. Optimization raised mean engagement from 0.30 to 0.70, with over two-thirds of seats reaching high engagement and back-row low-activity patterns markedly reduced. These results show that, without hardware changes, interpretable, data-driven seating strategies can substantially enhance engagement. The multimodal "assessment + optimization" paradigm offers a transferable, sustainable path to culturally responsive, differentiated spatial design amid global homogenization.

</details>


### [281] [EMAS: Stabilizing Multi-Agent System Evolution through Evidence-Guided Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07196)
*Chao Fei,Qingyi Si,Kaihua Liang,Yanghua Xiao,Panos Kalnis,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EMAS是一种利用运行经验自动迭代演化多智能体系统(MAS)拓扑和提示词的方法，无需更新LLM参数，既能提升准确率又能降低token成本。它在四个基准测试和两个LLM上达到最高的任务加权平均准确率，并在两个演化周期内分别获得6.30%和20.10%的相对准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化多智能体系统设计方法通常在初始设计阶段优化提示词和拓扑后即固定部署，无法将从新样本中获得的经验整合为可复用的系统更新。同时，追求准确率的设计往往带来高昂的token成本。因此需要一种能够持续利用运行经验来改进MAS的系统。

Method: EMAS的核心流程包括：(1) 将运行轨迹(traces)转换为结构化诊断结果，指明具体的修订操作和目标；(2) 仅在相同诊断在多个样本中重复出现时生成候选修订；(3) 通过当前MAS的配对验证，仅当满足相应验收标准时才应用修订。整个过程不更新LLM参数，只修订MAS拓扑和提示词。

Result: 在四个基准测试和两个LLM上，EMAS在两种骨干网络上均取得最高的任务加权整体准确率，在八个模型-基准组合中有六个达到最佳或持平。在两个演化周期内，EMAS在Kimi-K2-6和Qwen3.6-27B上分别实现了6.30%和20.10%的任务加权准确率相对提升。在MBPP+Qwen3.6-27B组合上，准确率从55.09%提升至89.12%，同时每任务token使用量降低62.2%。

Conclusion: EMAS证明了将新样本的运行经验转化为MAS拓扑和提示词的可复用更新是可行且有效的，在显著提升准确率的同时大幅降低token成本。

Abstract: Many methods for automated multi-agent system design optimize prompts and topologies during an initial design stage and then deploy the resulting system unchanged on subsequent samples. Experience from these samples is rarely consolidated into reusable system updates, while accuracy-oriented designs may incur high token costs. We introduce EMAS (Evolving Multi-Agent System), which uses this experience to revise MAS topology and prompts without updating LLM parameters, either to improve accuracy or to reduce cost. EMAS converts traces into structured diagnoses that specify a revision operation and target. It generates a candidate revision only when the same diagnosis recurs across samples and applies it only if paired validation against the current MAS meets the corresponding acceptance criterion. Across four benchmarks and two LLMs, EMAS attains the highest task-weighted overall accuracy for both backbones and is best or tied in six of eight model--benchmark settings. Within two evolution epochs, EMAS achieves relative gains of 6.30% and 20.10% in task-weighted accuracy on Kimi-K2-6 and Qwen3.6-27B, respectively. On MBPP with Qwen3.6-27B, EMAS raises accuracy from 55.09% to 89.12% while reducing token use per task by 62.2%. These results show that EMAS can turn experience from new samples into reusable updates to MAS topology and prompts.

</details>


### [282] [Authoring and Management of Transparent Research Integrity Assessments of Randomised Clinical Trial Publications Using LLM-assisted Tools and Provenance Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07202)
*Milan Markovic,Goutham Indukuri,Somayajulu Sripada,Colby J. Vorland,Jack Wilkinson,Clare Robertson,Mark Bolland,Andrew Grey,Miriam Brazzelli,Alison Avenell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了INSPECT-AI，一个基于大型语言模型（LLM）的交互式工具，用于协助人类审稿人对已发表的随机对照试验（RCTs）进行研究诚信评估。该工具基于社区认可的INSPECT-SR框架，并使用RIPE-O本体记录评估过程。同时，作者构建了RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含140项专家对95篇RCT论文的研究诚信评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCT的系统综述是临床护理指南的重要证据来源，必须满足高标准的研究诚信要求，以防止低质量或虚假的研究影响临床实践。然而，评估已发表RCT的研究诚信是一个复杂的过程，需要大量人工努力，且不同评估者可能得出不同结论。

Method: 开发了INSPECT-AI这一基于LLM的交互式工具，基于社区认证的INSPECT-SR框架辅助人类审稿人进行RCT研究诚信评估，并使用RIPE-O本体（Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology）记录评估过程的溯源信息。同时构建了RIPE-KG知识图谱，包含140项由INSPECT-AI生成的专家研究诚信评估，涵盖了95篇RCT出版物。

Result: 构建了RIPE-KG，即一个初始的知识图谱，包含140项专家对95篇RCT出版物（由INSPECT-AI生成）的研究诚信评估，均使用RIPE-O本体描述。

Conclusion: INSPECT-AI将LLM能力与人类专业知识相结合，为RCT研究诚信评估提供了交互式辅助工具，并通过RIPE-O本体和RIPE-KG知识图谱实现了评估过程的溯源记录与数据共享，有望提高研究诚信评估的效率与一致性。

Abstract: Systematic reviews of Randomised Controlled Trials (RCTs) are routinely used as evidence for clinical care guidelines. Such evidence has to meet high research integrity standards to prevent low quality or false research outputs influencing the clinical care. However, assessing research integrity of published RCTs is a complex process requiring manual effort, and potentially resulting in diverse opinions of the human assessors. This paper describes INSPECT-AI, an LLM-based interactive tool that assists human reviewers with research integrity assessments of published RCTs based on the community approved INSPECT-SR framework, and the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology (RIPE-O) for documenting the provenance of the assessment process. In addition, we present the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence knowledge graph (RIPE-KG), an initial set of 140 expert research integrity assessments of 95 RCT publications generated by INSPECT-AI and described using RIPE-O.

</details>


### [283] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretable Models of Human Randomisation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07220)
*Ngoc Linh Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用84,060个决策（来自2,802对玩家）研究O'Neill零和纸牌游戏中人类玩家的非i.i.d行为。研究发现，与LSTM等黑箱序列模型相比，透明的行为模型（特别是捕捉重复/回避行为，尤其是玩家对自身近期行动历史的管理）能够解释大部分可解释且可利用的战略信号，而频率追踪在样本外几乎没有额外贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合策略均衡预测玩家行为是i.i.d的，但人类玩家系统性地偏离这一基准，且黑箱序列模型（如LSTM）能够预测这些偏离。本文的动机在于：这些预测能力能否由透明的、可解释的替代模型实现，从而揭示背后的行为结构。

Method: 使用来自2,802对玩家的84,060个决策数据，首先对朴素模型、行为模型、可解释机器学习模型和深度学习模型进行基准比较；然后评估已有工作中的修改版EWA（经验加权吸引）模型；利用LASSO诊断方法进一步提出嵌套的频率跟踪扩展模型。

Result: 重复/回避行为，尤其是玩家对自身近期行动历史的管理策略，构成了大部分可解释且可战略利用的信号；频率跟踪模型在样本外预测中几乎没有额外增益。

Conclusion: 透明、可解释的行为模型能够达到接近黑箱序列模型的预测能力，并揭示了人类偏离i.i.d基准的核心机制是玩家自身的重复/回避行为管理，而非频率追踪。

Abstract: Mixed strategy equilibrium predicts i.i.d play: past actions should not help predict future decisions. Human players, however, systematically depart from this benchmark, and in O'Neill's zero sum card game, these departures can be predicted by black box sequence models such as LSTMs. This paper asks whether that predictive power can be achieved by transparent alternatives that also reveal the behavioural structure behind it. Using 84,060 decisions from 2,802 pairs, the analysis first benchmarks naive and behavioral models against interpretable machine learning and deep learning models, then evaluates the modified EWA specifications of prior work against these benchmarks and uses the LASSO diagnostics to motivate a further nested frequency tracking extension. The results show that repeat or avoid behavior, especially players' management of their own recent action histories, accounts for most of the interpretable and strategically exploitable signal, while frequency tracking adds little out of sample.

</details>


### [284] [From probability to causality in probabilistic logic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07230)
*Zora Wurm,Kilian Rückschloß,Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了概率逻辑编程（PLP）中因果序唯一性的问题。当从数据中学习概率逻辑程序的结构时，仅使用概率信息可能导致多种兼容的因果序，从而引发干预推理的歧义。作者利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的关系，推导出概率信息唯一确定因果序的条件，并引入由底层关系词汇表诱导的因果对称性约束，最终提出了一种验证学习的PLP程序是否支持明确定义干预语义的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率逻辑编程作为统计关系人工智能的形式化方法，支持从外部干预的因果查询。然而，当从数据中学习程序结构时，仅利用概率信息可能导致单一概率分布兼容多种因果序，造成干预推理的歧义。因此需要确定因果序何时能被分布唯一决定的条件。

Method: 利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的等价关系，推导出概率信息唯一确定因果序的条件；同时整合关系结构约束，考虑由底层关系词汇表诱导的预设因果对称性集合，从而构建完整的验证方法。

Result: 提出了一个验证方法，能够判断学习到的概率逻辑程序是否具有明确定义的干预语义，即是否能在给定概率信息和关系结构约束下唯一确定因果序。

Conclusion: 该研究为概率逻辑编程中的因果推理提供了理论保障，使得从数据学习到的PLP程序在满足特定条件下支持明确定义的干预语义，解决了概率信息与因果序之间的歧义问题。

Abstract: Probabilistic logic programming is a formalism of statistical relational artificial intelligence that supports causal queries, including interventions from outside the system. When the structure of a probabilistic logic program is learned from data, however, only probabilistic information is used, and a single probability distribution may be compatible with several causal orders. This leads to ambiguity in interventional reasoning, raising the question of when the causal order is uniquely determined by the distribution. Exploiting the relationship between acyclic probabilistic logic programs and Bayesian networks, we derive conditions under which the probabilistic information encoded in a program determines a unique causal order. We also incorporate constraints arising from relational structure by taking into account prescribed sets of causal symmetries induced by the underlying relational vocabulary. The result is a method for verifying when a learned probabilistic logic program supports well-defined intervention semantics.

</details>


### [285] [WNM-3D: A World Navigation Model with 3D Scene Conditioning for Closed-Loop VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07267)
*Yuehao Huang,Yunzi Wu,Xiaotao Zhang,Xinhai Li,Jiankun Dong,Jiajun Lv,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Yong Liu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WNM-3D是一种用于连续视觉语言导航（VLN）的生成式世界导航模型，通过3D场景条件化来联合预测未来观测和导航动作。它利用冻结的几何编码器从单目RGB历史中提取几何感知表征，并通过可训练的3D场景到token适配器将这些表征转换为token空间中的固定长度前缀，以条件化世界-动作扩散Transformer的每个未来视频-动作块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言动作（VLA）策略虽然语义能力强，但仅进行以动作为中心的训练，未显式建模视觉观测在预测动作下的演化过程。现有生成式世界动作模型（WAMs）虽能联合预测未来观测和动作，但未将联合生成过程条件化于从历史观测中推断的几何感知表征，导致缺乏对场景几何结构的显式利用。

Method: WNM-3D采用三部分架构：1）冻结的前馈几何编码器从单目RGB历史中提取几何感知表征；2）可训练的3D场景到token适配器将几何表征转换为固定长度前缀；3）世界-动作扩散Transformer通过块因果注意力使该前缀条件化所有未来视频-动作块。训练采用三种策略：A*生成演示上的监督式世界-动作微调、DAgger风格策略访问状态自适应、以及DanceGRPO闭环策略优化。

Result: 在GN-Bench基准上，WNM-3D在闭环导航中优于强VLM导航策略及其2D条件化对应方法。在固定近目标评估集上，WNM-3D取得了更高的流动作一致性和更低的视觉-运动误差。

Conclusion: 3D几何感知场景条件化能够有效提升生成式世界导航模型在连续VLN中的表现，通过共享几何上下文统一条件化未来视图和动作生成，显著优于仅基于二维条件或纯语义动作策略的方法。

Abstract: Recent vision-language navigation (VLN) systems increasingly adapt pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into vision-language-action (VLA) policies that map egocentric observations and language instructions directly to navigation actions. Although semantically capable, such action-centric training does not explicitly model how the agent's visual observations should evolve under its predicted motion. Generative world-action models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, yet existing WAMs for continuous VLN do not condition joint future-view and action generation on geometry-aware representations inferred from the observed history. We present WNM-3D, a generative World Navigation Model with 3D scene conditioning for continuous VLN. To consolidate past observations into persistent scene context, a frozen feed-forward geometry encoder extracts geometry-aware representations from the monocular egocentric RGB history, and a trainable 3D Scene-to-Token Adapter converts them into a fixed-length prefix in the token space of the world-action Diffusion Transformer. Through block-causal attention, this prefix conditions every future video-action block, providing a shared geometric context for both future-view and action generation. We train WNM-3D through supervised world-action fine-tuning on A*-generated demonstrations, DAgger-style adaptation on policy-visited states, and DanceGRPO-based closed-loop policy optimization. Experiments on GN-Bench show that WNM-3D outperforms strong VLM-based navigation policies and its 2D-conditioned counterpart in closed-loop navigation. On a fixed near-goal evaluation set, WNM-3D also achieves higher flow-action consistency and lower visual-motion error.

</details>


### [286] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究揭示了短时间预算（数十秒而非数小时）下AutoML系统比较中常见的协议缺陷。作者发现其简单AutoML引擎Orcetra在名义60秒预算下看似击败FLAML和AutoGluon，但胜利源于两个协议缺陷：测试集被用于候选评估导致指标膨胀，以及预算检查未在候选执行期间强制导致实际耗时超预算。修正协议后Orcetra胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，且无显著差异。作者还在同一搜索中记录两种估计目标以量化偏差来源，并提出短预算比较的检查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AutoML系统在短时间预算下的比较在工具README和研讨会论文中非常常见且容易出错，但缺乏对协议缺陷的系统性分析。作者希望通过案例研究揭示看似显著的性能差异背后隐藏的协议问题，并为短预算比较提供方法论指导。

Method: 作者以Orcetra引擎为案例，在513个OpenML数据集上对比FLAML和AutoGluon。首先按常规（有缺陷的）协议进行基准测试，然后修正三个关键协议：将候选评估从测试集移到验证集、在外部强制执行截止时间、确保各框架获得相等的机器资源。此外，作者在单一搜索中同时记录训练集选择和验证集选择的两种估计目标，以归因性能崩溃的来源，并利用轨迹计算选择偏差随预算变化的函数。

Result: 按有缺陷的协议，Orcetra在60秒预算下胜率57.1%，30秒对FLAML胜率78.4%。修正协议后，Orcetra胜率从59.4%降至34.3%，无显著差异。归因分析显示：选择规则贡献4.8个百分点偏差，计算资源不均贡献其余大部分。测量到的选择偏差随K增长但仅达0.27精度点，约为边际标准误差论证预测的σ√(2lnK)边界的五分之一，因为共享测试行上的候选评分相互抵消了大部分噪声。

Conclusion: 短时间预算下的AutoML比较极易因协议缺陷而产生误导性结论。作者提出检查清单，强调必须将选择与评估分离（训练/验证 vs 测试）、严格执行预算截止时间、合理分配计算资源。该工作为短预算基准测试提供了可复现的方法论框架，并展示了协议修正如何彻底改变竞品排名。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [287] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A²E（Agent Auditing Engine），一个面向智能体框架（harness）的端到端评估引擎，通过Agent Task Protocol实现任务快速集成、通过自动插桩的Monitor捕获标准化执行轨迹，并以多维指标系统评估框架能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的快速发展，智能体框架成为部署智能体的关键基础设施，但现有评估缺乏端到端、系统化、全面的流水线，难以高效评估不同框架的能力差异。

Method: A²E包含三个核心组件：(1) Agent Task Protocol (ATP)协议，实现不同框架间评估任务的快速集成；(2) 自动插桩的Monitor，在实验过程中捕获并生成标准化执行轨迹；(3) 评估阶段使用多维指标套件，涵盖执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等维度，而不仅仅依赖正确率。

Result: 实验表明模型-框架组合在不同类型任务上表现出显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上一致地优于其他组合，验证了系统化评估的必要性。

Conclusion: A²E揭示了框架与模型协同演化的必要性，为智能体框架的系统化评估提供了实用指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [288] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QFCQT是一种量子分形启发的混沌门控Quantformer模型，通过可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块和光滑-混沌门控融合机制，在非平稳时间序列预测任务上优于多个强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临长程依赖、局部波动突变、结构性转变和非线性振荡行为等挑战。虽然Transformer类预测器擅长建模长期时间依赖，但其前馈模块通常依赖光滑静态激活函数，对突变的机制切换不够敏感，难以捕捉复杂波动动力学。

Method: QFCQT包含三个核心组件：(1) Quantformer风格的数字编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；(2) 可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应，并通过Max-over-Time池化汇总；(3) 光滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡常规光滑激活与混沌敏感响应。此外，使用八个参数化Lee振荡器族的软叠加（soft superposition）而非单一固定振荡器，以自适应捕捉不同机制的多种非线性响应模式。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股票指数三个基准数据集上的实验表明，QFCQT一致性地优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线模型。

Conclusion: 本文表明，将振荡器启发的非线性激活与混沌敏感的门控机制引入Transformer前馈模块，能够有效提升非平稳时间序列的鲁棒预测能力；同时特别指出"量子-分形"仅作为计算类比，并非严格的量子力学或分形理论推导。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [289] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"课程即代码"范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，将生成式AI与LaTeX/Python集成，自动化创建可复现、视觉一致且技术准确的STEM教学材料。经过一年在8个模块和28个项目场景中的验证，显著降低了教师工作量，获得了8.5-9.9/10的高质量评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为主动学习定制教学材料给教师带来沉重负担，现有演示工具对技术内容支持不足，而当前AI应用常产生幻觉且未能将教学设计过程形式化，限制了其在严谨学术设计中的实用性。本文旨在解决这一痛点。

Method: 采用六阶段流程管线取代临时提示词工程，以基于文本的界面和代码驱动生成为核心——用LaTeX/Beamer生成幻灯片、Python生成图表，由教学约束、上下文校准和自动化评审循环共同管控，从而保留教师隐性教学知识于显式规则中。

Result: 在基于项目式学习（PBL）环境中经过一年验证，覆盖8个模块和28个项目场景，显著降低了教师工作量；生成的资产通过独立同行评审，并已被6位不同教师部署使用；基于600多份学生自愿评价，材料质量评分达到8.5-9.9/10，展现出高可复现性、最小化幻觉以及持续的教学和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了其可行性，可广泛应用于STEM教育领域，表明通过系统化的工作流整合AI与传统技术工具，能够在保证质量的同时大幅减轻教师负担。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [290] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用欧洲社会调查数据，分析10个主流商业LLM在15个社会人口学变量和居住国维度上与人类价值观的一致性，发现LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐是不均衡的，国家作为单一变量能解释与全部社会人口变量相当的变化量，且二者在解释价值对齐模式上具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作信息和建议的主要来源，但现有关于LLM与人类价值观对齐的研究主要以国家或文化边界来定义研究人群，忽视了社会人口学差异在价值对齐偏差中可能发挥的作用。本文旨在填补这一知识空白。

Method: 基于欧洲社会调查数据，选取10个主流商业LLM，从15个社会人口学变量（教育、收入、职业、宗教等）以及居住国维度，分析LLM与不同人口群体的价值对齐程度；并通过个体层面的分析，解构国家和人口因素在解释对齐模式中的相对贡献。

Result: LLM对不同社会人口群体（尤其是教育、收入、职业和宗教界定的群体）的价值观对齐不均衡。个体层面分析显示，国家作为独立变量能解释相当大比例的变化，与全部社会人口变量之和相当。国家和人口因素在解释价值对齐模式上具有互补性，且两者的相对权重因问题子集而异。

Conclusion: LLM的价值观对齐不仅存在国家层面的差异，还存在显著的社会人口学层面的不均衡。国家与人口因素互补地共同影响价值对齐模式，这提示在评估和改进LLM价值对齐时，需要同时考虑国家和社会人口学维度，而非仅仅以国家或文化为边界。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [291] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个针对SEC文件检索的"证据敏感型"基准测试，包含22家公司10-K和10-Q文件中的1185条人工撰写的问答记录，评估系统在实体、报告期和披露上下文三个维度上的证据识别能力。基线结果显示即使7B指令微调嵌入模型在Recall@10上仅达44.8%，次十亿参数编码器相比BM25最多提升3.5点，而使用精心筛选的困难负样本后配对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点，表明该任务具有显著挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融问答评估仅关注答案正确性，但SEC文件中，即使数字正确的答案也可能依据错误的证据段落。相似事实和披露在文件各章节、同一公司的不同报告期以及可比公司之间反复出现，这导致现有的检索与问答系统容易混淆证据来源，需要一种能评估"证据正确性"的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: FinRank基准包含1185条人工撰写的问答记录，覆盖22家公司的10-K和10-Q文件。每条记录包含参考答案、黄金支持段落以及经过人工筛选的困难负样本（来自文件内、跨报告期和跨可比公司的混淆段落）。基准将段落检索、重排序和困难负样本判别作为三个独立任务分别评估。

Result: 基线实验显示：7B指令微调嵌入模型在合并证据语料上Recall@10仅为44.8%；次十亿参数编码器相对BM25最多提升3.5点；金融领域适配的嵌入模型比BM25低9.7点；将随机负样本替换为精心筛选的困难负样本后，配对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank证明了现有检索系统在金融文件证据识别方面存在显著不足，即使先进模型也难以在混淆段落中准确区分证据来源。该基准为开发不仅准确而且具备正确披露依据的金融问答系统提供了"证据优先"的评测框架。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [292] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa，一种计算均衡的测试时推理路由策略，将固定推理预算下的生成、验证和停止视为计算分配问题。在3129个评估上，CoBa以49.1%-58.9%更少的参数加权token达到与强基准相当的准确率（85.13%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法通常在单一维度上增加计算（更多采样、更长的思维链或更强的评估器），但在固定推理预算下这些选择互相竞争。本文希望解决如何在生成、验证和停止之间智能分配计算，实现更高效的测试时推理。

Method: 提出CoBa（Compute-Balanced routing policy）：首先生成少量候选解，应用廉价验证进行广泛筛选，然后对不确定或高价值候选路由到更强的验证器。这是一种计算均衡的路由策略，根据当前状态动态决定下一步计算应投入生成、验证还是停止。

Result: 在MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023及程序化符号推理共3129个评估上，CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%宏平均准确率，与85.20%的自评估加权投票代理统计匹配，但节省49.1%参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票在0.01精度内匹配，节省58.9% token。配对bootstrap检验显示相对单样本解码有显著增益。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展的核心问题不是投入更多计算，而是将下一个单位计算投放到最有价值的位置。CoBa展示了均衡路由策略能在大幅降低推理成本的同时匹配甚至接近更强的计算密集型基线，剩余与池oracle的差距表明更好的路由仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [293] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明视觉语言模型（VLM）通过将数值时间序列编码为二维图像，可在电信网络分析中实现3.6-10.4倍的输入token缩减和1.8-2.5倍的推理能耗降低，同时显著提升异常检测精度（相比纯文本模型提升220.7%），确立了VLM作为数值时序任务中兼顾能效与精度的首选模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理占AI运行能耗的90%以上，且能耗随输入token数量线性增长。电信网络数值时间序列分析（NTSDA）中，4G/5G小区的原始多元KPI窗口会膨胀为数千个浮点token，远超生产环境LLM的上下文窗口（如128K），导致文本处理不可行或需截断，造成巨大的能效瓶颈。

Method: 利用Vision-Language Models（VLMs）将时间序列数据编码为二维图像/图表表示，代替纯文本token序列。在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B三种架构上验证了该方法的有效性，并在电信异常检测任务上与文本模型及传统基线（LSTM、ARIMA）进行对比，同时在公共基准上评估J/F1分数。

Result: 视觉表示实现3.6-10.4倍输入token缩减和1.8-2.5倍推理能耗降低，在监控200小区/15分钟间隔的边缘部署场景下每日节省约7.2 MJ能量。微调的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision在电信异常检测中比文本版本精度高220.7%，比LSTM和ARIMA基线高144%以上。Pixtral-12B在公共基准上J/F1提升20.6倍（平均F1=0.82）。在24个KPI下文本表示超出128K上下文窗口，而视觉表示仍在标准范围内。

Conclusion: VLM是将能源消耗视为一等工程约束的AI推理系统中，数值时间序列工作负载的高能效且高精度模态解决方案，具有明确的实践经验支撑。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [294] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA——一种可撤销的证据-记忆机制，将"有效性"作为记忆的显式状态，通过键控先例（keyed precedents）和撤销机制解决语言智能体的记忆污染问题（过期记忆仍可被检索并污染提示）。实验表明TEPA在受控隐藏机制漂移、真实文件可执行漂移和偏好更新流中均显著优于追加式（append-only）和最后写入优先（last-write-wins）记忆策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言智能体能够复用过去的事实、偏好和任务经验，但持久性也带来了根本的可证伪性问题：当世界发生变化时，过时记忆仍可被检索并污染提示（即"记忆污染"）。现有的追加式记忆和最后写入优先策略无法有效处理被新证据推翻的旧记忆。

Method: TEPA将观察表示为带键的先例（precedents），当同一键下的新证据与旧先例矛盾时，主动撤销（revoke）旧的活跃先例。检索时从当前证据中提取，同时保留被撤销的历史以供审计。

Result: 在受控漂移的50个种子实验中，完全反转场景下追加式与最后写入优先策略均降至无记忆水平（均为0.210，无记忆为0.309），而TEPA达到0.950；在真实文件执行场景中复现相同模式（追加式0.203，无记忆0.298，TEPA 0.950）。在干净的MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强基线最后写入优先缓存持平。边界测试暴露了多跳和超长上下文场景中检索链与上下文选择方面的瓶颈。
